'use client';

import { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import { Modal, ModalHeader, ModalTitle, ModalBody } from '@/components/composites/Modal/Modal';
import { Button } from '@/components/primitives/Button/Button';
import { Select } from '@/components/primitives/Select/Select';
import { TextArea } from '@/components/primitives/TextArea/TextArea';
import { useGetReturnRequestReasons, useSubmitReturnRequest } from '@/domains/userpanel/hooks/userpanel.hooks';
import { toPersianDigits, getFullUrl } from '@/core/utils/formatters';
import { showToast } from '@/core/utils/toast';
import { Paperclip, FileText, X, AlertTriangle } from 'lucide-react';

interface ReturnRequestModalProps {
  isOpen: boolean;
  onClose: () => void;
  subOrder: any;
  item: any;
}

export function ReturnRequestModal({ isOpen, onClose, subOrder, item }: ReturnRequestModalProps) {
  const { data: reasons = [] } = useGetReturnRequestReasons();
  const submitReturn = useSubmitReturnRequest();

  const [selectedReasonId, setSelectedReasonId] = useState('');
  const [returnQuantity, setReturnQuantity] = useState(1);
  const [description, setDescription] = useState('');
  const [attachments, setAttachments] = useState<File[]>([]);

  // ? ??? ???? ??? ????? ??? ??? ?????
  useEffect(() => {
    if (isOpen) {
      setSelectedReasonId('');
      setReturnQuantity(1);
      setDescription('');
      setAttachments([]);
    }
  }, [isOpen]);

  const handleFileChange = (e: React.ChangeEvent<HTMLInputElement>) => {
    if (e.target.files) {
      const filesArray = Array.from(e.target.files);
      // ??????? ? ????? ? ????? ??? ????
      const validFiles = filesArray.filter(file => file.type.startsWith('image/'));
      const remainingSlots = 5 - attachments.length;
      
      if (validFiles.length > remainingSlots) {
        showToast.error(`??? ??? ????????? ${remainingSlots} ????? ???? ????? ????`);
      }
      
      setAttachments(prev => [...prev, ...validFiles].slice(0, 5));
    }
  };

  const handleRemoveFile = (idx: number) => {
    setAttachments(prev => prev.filter((_, i) => i !== idx));
  };

  const handleFormSubmit = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();

    if (!selectedReasonId) {
      showToast.error('????? ??? ???? ?????? ???? ?? ?????? ????');
      return;
    }

    if (description.trim().length < 10) {
      showToast.error('????? ???? ?????? ?? ?? ?????? ????? (????? ?? ???????) ????? ????');
      return;
    }

    try {
      const itemsPayload = [
        {
          subOrderItemId: item.id,
          quantity: returnQuantity,
          returnRequestReasonId: selectedReasonId,
          description: description,
        }
      ];

      await submitReturn.mutateAsync({
        subOrderId: subOrder.id,
        items: itemsPayload,
        files: attachments,
      });

      showToast.success('??????? ?????? ??? ?? ?????? ??? ?? ? ?? ??? ?????? ????? ?????');
      onClose();
    } catch (error: any) {
      showToast.error(error.userMessage || '??? ?? ??? ??????? ?????? ????');
    }
  };

  const quantityOptions = Array.from({ length: item?.quantity || 1 }, (_, i) => ({
    value: String(i + 1),
    label: `${toPersianDigits(i + 1)} ???`,
  }));

  if (!item) return null;

  return (
    <Modal isOpen={isOpen} onClose={onClose} className="max-w-md w-full">
      <ModalHeader onClose={onClose}>
        <ModalTitle className="font-bold text-sm text-foreground flex items-center gap-2">
          <AlertTriangle className="h-5 w-5 text-destructive" />
          ??? ??????? ?????? ????
        </ModalTitle>
      </ModalHeader>

      <ModalBody className="p-5 pt-4">
        <form onSubmit={handleFormSubmit} className="flex flex-col gap-4.5 w-full">
          
          {/* ??????? ????? ???? */}
          <div className="flex items-center gap-3 bg-muted/30 p-3 rounded-xl border">
            <div className="w-12 h-12 shrink-0 rounded-lg border bg-card flex items-center justify-center overflow-hidden p-0.5">
              <img src={getFullUrl(item.shopProduct.product.image)} className="w-full h-full object-contain" alt="" />
            </div>
            <div className="flex-1 min-w-0">
              <span className="text-xs font-bold text-foreground truncate block">{item.shopProduct.product.title}</span>
              <span className="text-[10px] text-muted-foreground block mt-0.5">???????: {subOrder.shop?.shopTitle}</span>
            </div>
          </div>

          {/* ???? ?????? */}
          <div className="w-full">
            <Select
              label="??? ???? ?????? ???? *"
              placeholder="??? ?????? ?? ?????? ????..."
              value={selectedReasonId}
              onChange={(e) => setSelectedReasonId(e.target.value)}
              options={reasons.map((r: any) => ({ value: r.id, label: r.name }))}
              required
            />
          </div>

          {/* ????? ?????? */}
          {item.quantity > 1 && (
            <div className="w-full">
              <Select
                label="????? ????? ?????? *"
                value={String(returnQuantity)}
                onChange={(e) => setReturnQuantity(Number(e.target.value))}
                options={quantityOptions}
                required
              />
            </div>
          )}

          {/* ??? ?????? */}
          <div className="w-full">
            <TextArea
              label="??? ??? ? ?????? ?????? ???? *"
              placeholder="????? ??????? ???? ?? ?????????? ??? ???? ?? ?? ??? ??? ???????..."
              value={description}
              onChange={(e) => setDescription(e.target.value)}
              className="h-24 text-xs"
              required
            />
          </div>

          {/* ????? ? ????? ??? */}
          <div className="flex flex-col gap-2 w-full border-t border-dashed pt-4">
            <span className="text-xs font-bold text-foreground mb-1">?????? ????? ????? ?? ????? (?????? ? ?????)</span>
            <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2.5 items-center">
              <label className="h-10 px-4 rounded-xl border border-dashed border-primary/30 hover:border-primary text-primary hover:bg-primary/5 text-xs font-bold flex items-center justify-center gap-1.5 cursor-pointer shadow-sm transition-all select-none">
                <Paperclip className="h-4 w-4" />
                <span>?????? ??? ????</span>
                <input
                  type="file"
                  multiple
                  accept="image/*"
                  onChange={handleFileChange}
                  className="hidden"
                />
              </label>

              {attachments.map((file, idx) => (
                <div 
                  key={idx}
                  className="h-10 px-2.5 rounded-xl border bg-muted/20 text-[9px] font-bold text-foreground flex items-center gap-1.5 max-w-[120px] shrink-0"
                >
                  <FileText className="h-3.5 w-3.5 text-primary shrink-0" />
                  <span className="truncate flex-1">{file.name}</span>
                  <button
                    type="button"
                    onClick={() => handleRemoveFile(idx)}
                    className="p-0.5 hover:bg-muted rounded-full flex items-center justify-center text-muted-foreground hover:text-destructive transition-colors shrink-0"
                  >
                    <X className="h-3 w-3" />
                  </button>
                </div>
              ))}
            </div>
          </div>

        </form>
      </ModalBody>

      <div className="flex flex-row gap-2.5 p-5 pt-4 border-t border-border/50 w-full shrink-0">
        <Button
          type="button"
          variant="outline"
          onClick={onClose}
          className="flex-1 rounded-xl text-xs h-10 font-bold"
        >
          ??????
        </Button>
        <Button
          type="submit"
          variant="primary"
          isLoading={submitReturn.isPending}
          className="flex-1 rounded-xl text-xs h-10 font-bold"
          onClick={handleFormSubmit}
        >
          ??? ??????? ??????
        </Button>
      </div>
    </Modal>
  );
}

'use client';

import * as React from 'react';
import { Modal, ModalHeader, ModalTitle, ModalBody } from '@/components/composites/Modal/Modal';
import { Button } from '@/components/primitives/Button/Button';
import { Input } from '@/components/primitives/Input/Input';
import { Select } from '@/components/primitives/Select/Select';
import { useSubmitReturnShipmentReceipt } from '@/domains/userpanel/hooks/userpanel.hooks';
import { showToast } from '@/core/utils/toast';
import { Truck, UploadCloud, CheckCircle, FileText, X } from 'lucide-react';

interface ReturnShipmentModalProps {
  isOpen: boolean;
  onClose: () => void;
  returnRequestId: string;
}

const SHIPMENT_METHODS = [
  { value: 'Tipax', label: 'تیپاکس' },
  { value: 'Post', label: 'پست پیشتاز' },
  { value: 'Carrier', label: 'باربری / چاپار' },
  { value: 'Direct', label: 'پیک / تحویل مستقیم' },
];

export function ReturnShipmentModal({ isOpen, onClose, returnRequestId }: ReturnShipmentModalProps) {
  const submitReceipt = useSubmitReturnShipmentReceipt();

  const [method, setMethod] = React.useState('Tipax');
  const [traceNumber, setTraceNumber] = React.useState('');
  const [file, setFile] = React.useState<File | null>(null);

  React.useEffect(() => {
    if (isOpen) {
      setMethod('Tipax');
      setTraceNumber('');
      setFile(null);
    }
  }, [isOpen]);

  const handleFileChange = (e: React.ChangeEvent<HTMLInputElement>) => {
    if (e.target.files && e.target.files[0]) {
      setFile(e.target.files[0]);
    }
  };

  const handleSubmit = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();

    if (!traceNumber.trim()) {
      showToast.error('لطفاً شماره رهگیری بارنامه پستی را وارد کنید');
      return;
    }

    if (!file) {
      showToast.error('لطفاً تصویر فیش یا بارنامه ارسال را بارگذاری کنید');
      return;
    }

    try {
      await submitReceipt.mutateAsync({
        id: returnRequestId,
        method,
        traceNumber: traceNumber.trim(),
        file,
      });
      showToast.success('اطلاعات بارنامه با موفقیت ثبت شد و روند بازگشت وجه آغاز گردید');
      onClose();
    } catch (err: any) {
      showToast.error(err?.userMessage || 'خطا در ثبت اطلاعات بارنامه پستی');
    }
  };

  return (
    <Modal isOpen={isOpen} onClose={onClose} className="w-full max-w-md">
      <ModalHeader onClose={onClose}>
        <ModalTitle className="flex items-center gap-2 text-sm font-bold text-foreground">
          <Truck className="h-5 w-5 text-primary" />
          <span>ثبت مشخصات ارسال کالای مرجوعی</span>
        </ModalTitle>
      </ModalHeader>

      <ModalBody className="p-5 pt-3">
        <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} className="flex w-full flex-col gap-4">
          <div className="rounded-xl border border-primary/20 bg-primary/5 p-3 text-xs leading-relaxed text-foreground">
            پس از تحویل کالا به پست یا تیپاکس، شماره بارنامه و تصویر قبض ارسال را در این بخش ثبت کنید تا بررسی شود.
          </div>

          <div className="w-full">
            <Select
              label="شیوه ارسال مرسوله *"
              value={method}
              onChange={(e) => setMethod(e.target.value)}
              options={SHIPMENT_METHODS}
              required
            />
          </div>

          <div className="w-full">
            <label className="mb-1 block text-2xs font-bold text-muted-foreground">کد یا شماره رهگیری بارنامه *</label>
            <Input
              placeholder="مثال: ۲۴۴۵۸۹۶۳۱۰۰۲"
              value={traceNumber}
              onChange={(e) => setTraceNumber(e.target.value)}
              dir="ltr"
              className="h-10 text-xs "
              required
            />
          </div>

          {/* بارگذاری تصویر فیش */}
          <div className="flex w-full flex-col gap-2">
            <span className="text-2xs font-bold text-muted-foreground">تصویر قبض پستی یا رسید باربری *</span>
            {!file ? (
              <label className="flex h-24 w-full cursor-pointer flex-col items-center justify-center rounded-xl border border-dashed border-primary/40 bg-card p-4 text-center transition-colors hover:border-primary hover:bg-primary/5">
                <UploadCloud className="h-6 w-6 text-primary mb-1" />
                <span className="text-xs font-bold text-foreground">برای انتخاب عکس فیش کلیک کنید</span>
                <span className="text-2xs text-muted-foreground mt-0.5">فرمت‌های مجاز: JPG, PNG</span>
                <input type="file" accept="image/*" onChange={handleFileChange} className="hidden" />
              </label>
            ) : (
              <div className="flex items-center justify-between rounded-xl border border-success/30 bg-success/5 p-3">
                <div className="flex items-center gap-2 min-w-0 flex-1">
                  <FileText className="h-5 w-5 text-success shrink-0" />
                  <span className="truncate text-xs font-semibold text-foreground">{file.name}</span>
                </div>
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => setFile(null)}
                  className="rounded-full p-1 text-muted-foreground hover:bg-secondary hover:text-destructive"
                >
                  <X className="h-4 w-4" />
                </button>
              </div>
            )}
          </div>

          <div className="mt-2 flex w-full gap-3 border-t border-border/60 pt-4">
            <Button
              type="button"
              variant="outline"
              onClick={onClose}
              className="h-10 flex-1 rounded-xl text-xs font-bold"
            >
              انصراف
            </Button>
            <Button
              type="submit"
              variant="primary"
              isLoading={submitReceipt.isPending}
              className="h-10 flex-1 rounded-xl text-xs font-bold shadow-xs"
            >
              ثبت نهایی بارنامه
            </Button>
          </div>
        </form>
      </ModalBody>
    </Modal>
  );
}

FILE: C:\Users\Raven\final-projects\yadakchi-front\yadakchi\src\components\features\Profile\components\WithdrawModal.tsx
--------------------------------------------------------

'use client';

import { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import { useForm } from 'react-hook-form';
import { zodResolver } from '@hookform/resolvers/zod';
import { z } from 'zod';
import { 
  useGetBankAccounts, 
  useSubmitWithdrawRequest 
} from '@/domains/userpanel/hooks/userpanel.hooks';
import { Modal, ModalHeader, ModalTitle, ModalBody } from '@/components/composites/Modal/Modal';
import { Button } from '@/components/primitives/Button/Button';
import { Input } from '@/components/primitives/Input/Input';
import { Select } from '@/components/primitives/Select/Select';
import { AddBankCardForm } from './AddBankCardForm';
import { CreditCard, Plus, ArrowRight } from 'lucide-react';
import { showToast } from '@/core/utils/toast';

const withdrawFormSchema = z.object({
  amount: z.number().int().positive('مبلغ برداشت باید عدد مثبت باشد'),
  bankAccountId: z.string().uuid('شناسه حساب بانکی نامعتبر است')
});

type WithdrawFormValues = z.infer<typeof withdrawFormSchema>;

interface WithdrawModalProps {
  isOpen: boolean;
  onClose: () => void;
  wallet: any;
}

export function WithdrawModal({ isOpen, onClose, wallet }: WithdrawModalProps) {
  const [isAddCardOpen, setIsAddCardOpen] = useState(false);

  const { data: bankAccounts = [] } = useGetBankAccounts();
  const submitWithdraw = useSubmitWithdrawRequest();

  const { register, handleSubmit, watch, reset, formState: { errors } } = useForm<WithdrawFormValues>({
    resolver: zodResolver(withdrawFormSchema),
    defaultValues: {
      amount: 0,
      bankAccountId: ''
    }
  });

  const watchAmount = watch('amount');

  useEffect(() => {
    if (isOpen) {
      const defaultAccountId = bankAccounts.find(b => b.isDefault)?.id || bankAccounts[0]?.id || '';
      reset({
        amount: 0,
        bankAccountId: defaultAccountId
      });
      setIsAddCardOpen(false);
    }
  }, [isOpen, bankAccounts, reset]);

  const handleWithdrawSubmit = async (data: WithdrawFormValues) => {
    const rawWithdrawable = wallet ? Number(wallet.withdrawableBalance.replace(/[^0-9]/g, '')) : 0;
    if (data.amount > rawWithdrawable) {
      showToast.error('مبلغ درخواستی بیشتر از موجودی قابل برداشت شماست');
      return;
    }

    try {
      await submitWithdraw.mutateAsync({
        amount: data.amount * 10,
        bankAccountId: data.bankAccountId
      });
      showToast.success('درخواست برداشت با موفقیت ثبت شد');
      onClose();
    } catch (error) {}
  };

  const formatNumber = (value: number) => {
    return new Intl.NumberFormat('fa-IR').format(value);
  };

  return (
 <Modal isOpen={isOpen} onClose={onClose} className="max-w-md w-full">
  <ModalHeader onClose={onClose}>
    <ModalTitle className=" font-bold text-sm text-foreground flex items-center gap-2">
      <CreditCard className="h-5 w-5 text-primary" />
      ثبت درخواست برداشت وجه
    </ModalTitle>
  </ModalHeader>
  
  <ModalBody className="p-5 pt-4">
    {!isAddCardOpen ? (
      <form onSubmit={handleSubmit(handleWithdrawSubmit)} className="flex flex-col gap-4 w-full">
        
        <div className="w-full flex items-center justify-between bg-primary/5 border border-primary/20 rounded-xl p-3.5 text-xs text-primary font-bold">
          <span>موجود قابل برداشت شما:</span>
          <span>{wallet?.withdrawableBalance || '۰ تومان'}</span>
        </div>

        <div className="w-full">
          {bankAccounts.length > 0 ? (
            <div className="w-full flex flex-col gap-2">
              <Select
                label="انتخاب کارت بانکی مقصد *"
                placeholder="یک کارت بانکی را انتخاب کنید..."
                error={errors.bankAccountId?.message ? String(errors.bankAccountId.message) : undefined}
                options={bankAccounts.map((b: any) => ({
                  value: b.id,
                  label: `${b.cardNumber} ${b.isDefault ? '(پیش‌فرض)' : ''}`
                }))}
                {...register('bankAccountId')}
              />
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => setIsAddCardOpen(true)}
                className="text-[10px] font-bold text-primary hover:underline self-end flex items-center gap-1 mt-1 outline-none"
              >
                <Plus className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
                <span>افزودن کارت بانکی جدید</span>
              </button>
            </div>
          ) : (
            <div className="w-full py-6 border border-dashed rounded-xl flex flex-col items-center justify-center gap-3 bg-muted/10">
              <span className="text-xs font-bold text-muted-foreground ">هیچ کارت بانکی ثبت نکرده‌اید.</span>
              <Button
                type="button"
                variant="outline"
                size="sm"
                onClick={() => setIsAddCardOpen(true)}
                className="rounded-xl font-bold text-xs h-9 px-4 flex items-center justify-center gap-1 shadow-sm"
              >
                <Plus className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
                <span>ثبت اولین کارت بانکی</span>
              </Button>
            </div>
          )}
        </div>

        <div className="w-full">
          <Input
            label="مبلغ برداشت دلخواه (تومان) *"
            placeholder="مبلغ مورد نظر خود را به تومان وارد کنید..."
            type="number"
            error={errors.amount?.message ? String(errors.amount.message) : undefined}
            rightIcon={<span className="text-xs font-bold text-muted-foreground ">تومان</span>}
            className="text-xs"
            dir="ltr"
            {...register('amount', { valueAsNumber: true })}
          />
          {watchAmount > 0 && (
            <span className="text-[10px] font-bold text-muted-foreground mt-1 block">
              معادل {formatNumber(Number(watchAmount) * 10)} ریال به بانک مقصد ارسال خواهد شد.
            </span>
          )}
        </div>

      </form>
    ) : (
      <div className="w-full flex flex-col gap-4 animate-in slide-in-from-left duration-200">
        <div className="flex items-center gap-2 border-b pb-2">
          <button 
            type="button" 
            onClick={() => setIsAddCardOpen(false)}
            className="p-1 hover:bg-muted rounded-full transition-colors"
            aria-label="مرحله قبل"
          >
            <ArrowRight className="h-4.5 w-4.5" />
          </button>
          <span className="text-xs font-bold text-foreground ">ثبت کارت بانکی جدید</span>
        </div>

        <AddBankCardForm onCancel={() => setIsAddCardOpen(false)} />
      </div>
    )}
  </ModalBody>

  {/* فوتر با دکمه‌های تمام عرض - فقط در حالت اصلی نمایش داده شود */}
  {!isAddCardOpen && (
    <div className="flex flex-row gap-2.5 p-5 pt-4 border-t border-border/50 w-full shrink-0">
      <Button
        type="button"
        variant="outline"
        onClick={onClose}
        className="flex-1 rounded-xl text-xs h-10 font-bold "
      >
        انصراف
      </Button>
      <Button
        type="submit"
        variant="primary"
        disabled={bankAccounts.length === 0}
        isLoading={submitWithdraw.isPending}
        className="flex-1 rounded-xl text-xs h-10 font-bold "
        onClick={handleSubmit(handleWithdrawSubmit)}
      >
        ثبت درخواست برداشت
      </Button>
    </div>
  )}
</Modal>
  );
}

  onClose: () => void;

  editingComment: any;
}

export function WriteCommentModal({ isOpen, onClose, product, editingComment }: WriteCommentModalProps) {
  const queryClient = useQueryClient();
  const createComment = useCreateComment();
  const updateComment = useUpdateComment();

  const [rate, setRate] = useState(5);
  const [hoverRate, setHoverRate] = useState(0);
  const [isIncognito, setIsIncognito] = useState(false);

  const { register, handleSubmit, reset, formState: { errors } } = useForm<WriteCommentFormValues>({
    resolver: zodResolver(
      z.object({
        comment: z.string().min(10, '??? ??? ???? ????? ?? ??????? ????').max(1000, '??? ??? ????? ?????? ???')
      })
    ),
    defaultValues: {
      comment: ''
    }
  });

  useEffect(() => {
    if (isOpen) {
      if (editingComment) {
        setRate(editingComment.rate);
        setIsIncognito(editingComment.isIncognito || false);
        reset({
          comment: editingComment.comment
        });
      } else {
        setRate(5);
        setIsIncognito(false);
        reset({
          comment: ''
        });
      }
    }
  }, [isOpen, editingComment, reset]);

  const onCommentSubmit = async (data: WriteCommentFormValues) => {

          id: editingComment.id,
          rate: rate
        });
        showToast.success('??????? ??? ??? ?? ?????? ??? ??');
      } else {
        await createComment.mutateAsync({
          productId: product.productId,
          comment: data.comment,
          rate: rate,
          isIncognito: isIncognito
        });
        showToast.success('????? ??????? ??? ?? ?????? ??? ??');
      }
      queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ['user', 'comments'] });
      onClose();
    } catch (error) {
      console.error('[WriteCommentModal] Submission failed:', error);
    }
  };

   <Modal isOpen={isOpen} onClose={onClose} className="max-w-md w-full">

'use client';

import * as React from 'react';
import { useRouter } from 'next/navigation';
import { useForm } from 'react-hook-form';
import { zodResolver } from '@hookform/resolvers/zod';
import { z } from 'zod';
import { useQueryClient } from '@tanstack/react-query';
import {
  MapPin,
  Plus,
  Trash2,
  Pencil,
  User,
  Phone,
  CheckCircle2,
  Navigation,
} from 'lucide-react';
import {
  useGetUserLocations,
  useCreateUserLocation,
  useUpdateUserLocation,
  useDeleteUserLocation,
} from '@/domains/front/basket/hooks/basket.hooks';
import { Card } from '@/components/composites/Card';
import { Button } from '@/components/primitives/Button/Button';
import { Input } from '@/components/primitives/Input/Input';
import { Modal, ModalHeader, ModalTitle, ModalBody } from '@/components/composites/Modal/Modal';
import { PageLoading } from '@/components/composites/Loading/PageLoading';
import { AddressMapModal } from '@/components/composites/AddressMapModal/AddressMapModal';
import { LottiePlayer } from '@/components/shared/Lottie/LottiePlayer';
import { showToast } from '@/core/utils/toast';
import { toPersianDigits } from '@/core/utils/formatters';
import { cn } from '@/design-system/utils/cn';

const addressFormSchema = z.object({
  title: z.string().min(2, 'عنوان آدرس باید حداقل ۲ کاراکتر باشد'),
  postalCode: z.string().regex(/^[0-9]{9,10}$/, 'کد پستی باید ۹ یا ۱۰ رقم باشد'),
  plaque: z.string().min(1, 'شماره پلاک الزامی است'),
  unit: z.string().min(1, 'شماره واحد الزامی است'),
  isUserReceiver: z.boolean(),
  receiverFullName: z.string().optional(),
  receiverMobile: z.string().optional(),
  receiverNationalCode: z.string().optional(),
});

type AddressFormValues = z.infer<typeof addressFormSchema>;

export function AddressesList() {
  const router = useRouter();
const queryClient = useQueryClient();
  const { data: locations = [], isLoading } = useGetUserLocations();

  const createLocation = useCreateUserLocation();
  const updateLocation = useUpdateUserLocation();
  const deleteLocation = useDeleteUserLocation();

  const [isMapOpen, setIsMapOpen] = React.useState(false);
  const [isFormOpen, setIsAddFormOpen] = React.useState(false);
  const [editingAddress, setEditingAddress] = React.useState<any | null>(null);
  const [mapInitialCoords, setMapInitialCoords] = React.useState<{ lat: number; lon: number } | null>(null);

  const [mapData, setMapAddressData] = React.useState<{
    address: string;
    latitude: number;
    longitude: number;
    cityId: string;
    cityName: string;
    provinceName: string;
  } | null>(null);

  const {
    register,
    handleSubmit,
    watch,
    reset,
    formState: { errors },
  } = useForm<AddressFormValues>({
    resolver: zodResolver(addressFormSchema),
    defaultValues: {
      title: '',
      postalCode: '',
      plaque: '',
      unit: '',
      isUserReceiver: true,
      receiverFullName: '',
      receiverMobile: '',
      receiverNationalCode: '',
    },
  });

  const watchIsUserReceiver = watch('isUserReceiver');

  const handleOpenNewAddressMap = () => {
    setEditingAddress(null);
    setMapInitialCoords(null);
    setIsMapOpen(true);
  };

  const handleOpenEditAddressMap = (addr: any) => {
    setEditingAddress(addr);
    setMapInitialCoords({ lat: addr.latitude, lon: addr.longitude });
    setIsMapOpen(true);
  };

  const handleConfirmMapAddress = (data: {
    address: string;
    latitude: number;
    longitude: number;
    cityId: string;
    cityName: string;
    provinceName: string;
  }) => {
    setMapAddressData(data);
    setIsMapOpen(false);

    if (editingAddress) {
      reset({
        title: editingAddress.title,
        postalCode: editingAddress.postalCode,
        plaque: editingAddress.plaque,
        unit: editingAddress.unit,
        isUserReceiver: editingAddress.isUserReceiver,
        receiverFullName: editingAddress.receiverFullName || '',
        receiverMobile: editingAddress.receiverMobile || '',
        receiverNationalCode: editingAddress.receiverNationalCode || '',
      });
    } else {
      reset({
        title: '',
        postalCode: '',
        plaque: '',
        unit: '',
        isUserReceiver: true,
        receiverFullName: '',
        receiverMobile: '',
        receiverNationalCode: '',
      });
    }
    setIsAddFormOpen(true);
  };

  const handleFormSubmit = async (data: AddressFormValues) => {
    if (!mapData) return;

    const formData = new FormData();
    formData.append('Title', data.title);
    formData.append('Address', mapData.address);
    formData.append('PostalCode', data.postalCode);
    formData.append('Plaque', data.plaque);
    formData.append('Unit', data.unit);
    formData.append('CityId', mapData.cityId);
    formData.append('IsUserReceiver', String(data.isUserReceiver));
    formData.append('Latitude', String(mapData.latitude));
    formData.append('Longitude', String(mapData.longitude));

    if (!data.isUserReceiver) {
      formData.append('ReceiverFullName', data.receiverFullName || '');
      formData.append('ReceiverMobile', data.receiverMobile || '');
      formData.append('ReceiverNationalCode', data.receiverNationalCode || '');
    }

    try {
      if (editingAddress) {
        formData.append('Id', editingAddress.id);
        formData.append('IsDefault', String(editingAddress.isDefault));
        await updateLocation.mutateAsync(formData);
        showToast.success('آدرس با موفقیت ویرایش شد');
      } else {
        formData.append('IsDefault', 'false');
        await createLocation.mutateAsync(formData);
        showToast.success('آدرس جدید با موفقیت ثبت شد');
      }
      setIsAddFormOpen(false);
      setEditingAddress(null);
      setMapAddressData(null);
    } catch {}
  };

  const handleDelete = async (id: string) => {
    try {
      await deleteLocation.mutateAsync(id);
      showToast.success('آدرس با موفقیت حذف شد');
    } catch {}
  };

  // ✅ اصلاح اصلی: ارسال درخواست PUT استاندارد به جای تغییر آدرس سبد خرید
 const handleSetDefault = async (loc: any) => {
    // ۱. تغییر آنی در کش (Optimistic Update) تا بدون ۱ میلی‌ثانیه تاخیر نتیجه را ببینید
    queryClient.setQueryData(['user', 'locations'], (old: any) => {
      if (!Array.isArray(old)) return old;
      return old.map((item: any) => ({
        ...item,
        isDefault: item.id === loc.id,
      }));
    });

    const formData = new FormData();
    formData.append('Id', loc.id);
    formData.append('Title', loc.title);
    formData.append('Address', loc.address);
    formData.append('PostalCode', loc.postalCode);
    formData.append('Plaque', loc.plaque);
    formData.append('Unit', loc.unit);
    formData.append('CityId', loc.cityId || '');
    formData.append('IsUserReceiver', String(loc.isUserReceiver));
    formData.append('Latitude', String(loc.latitude || 0));
    formData.append('Longitude', String(loc.longitude || 0));
    formData.append('IsDefault', 'true');

    if (!loc.isUserReceiver) {
      formData.append('ReceiverFullName', loc.receiverFullName || '');
      formData.append('ReceiverMobile', loc.receiverMobile || '');
      formData.append('ReceiverNationalCode', loc.receiverNationalCode || '');
    }

    try {
      await updateLocation.mutateAsync(formData);
      showToast.success('آدرس پیش‌فرض تحویل سفارشات تغییر یافت');
      // ۲. دریافت لیست تازه از سرور
      await queryClient.refetchQueries({ queryKey: ['user', 'locations'] });
    } catch {
      // در صورت بروز خطا لیست را به حالت قبل بازمی‌گردانیم
      await queryClient.refetchQueries({ queryKey: ['user', 'locations'] });
      showToast.error('خطا در تغییر آدرس پیش‌فرض');
    }
  };
  if (isLoading) {
    return <PageLoading label="در حال دریافت لیست آدرس‌های شما..." />;
  }

  return (
    <div className="flex w-full flex-col gap-5" dir="rtl">
      
      {/* هدر بخش آدرس‌ها */}
      <div className="flex items-center justify-between border-b border-border/70 pb-4">
        <div className="flex flex-col gap-1">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
            <MapPin className="h-5 w-5 text-primary" />
            <h1 className="text-base font-black text-foreground sm:text-lg">آدرس‌های من</h1>
          </div>
          <p className="hidden text-xs text-muted-foreground sm:block">
            آدرس‌های ثبت‌شده جهت تسریع در ارسال سفارش‌ها و محاسبه دقیق هزینه حمل‌ونقل
          </p>
        </div>

        <Button
          variant="primary"
          size="sm"
          onClick={handleOpenNewAddressMap}
          className="h-9 gap-1.5 rounded-xl px-4 text-xs font-bold shadow-xs"
        >
          <Plus className="h-4 w-4" />
          <span>ثبت آدرس جدید</span>
        </Button>
      </div>

      {/* لیست آدرس‌ها */}
      {locations.length > 0 ? (
        <div className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-4 md:grid-cols-2">
          {locations.map((loc: any) => (
            <Card
              key={loc.id}
              className={cn(
                'flex flex-col justify-between rounded-2xl border p-4.5 sm:p-5 shadow-xs transition-all',
                loc.isDefault ? 'border-primary/50 bg-primary/2' : 'border-border/80 bg-card'
              )}
            >
              <div>
                {/* ردیف تایتل و دکمه پیش‌فرض */}
                <div className="flex items-center justify-between border-b border-border/50 pb-3">
                  <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                    <span className="text-sm font-black text-foreground">{loc.title}</span>
                    {loc.isDefault && (
                      <span className="flex items-center gap-1 rounded-md bg-emerald-500/10 px-2 py-0.5 text-3xs font-bold text-emerald-600 dark:text-emerald-400">
                        <CheckCircle2 className="h-3 w-3" />
                        پیش‌فرض تحویل
                      </span>
                    )}
                  </div>

                  {!loc.isDefault && (
                    <button
                      type="button"
                      onClick={() => handleSetDefault(loc)}
                      disabled={updateLocation.isPending}
                      className="rounded-lg border border-border px-2.5 py-1 text-3xs font-bold text-muted-foreground hover:border-primary/40 hover:bg-accent hover:text-primary transition-colors disabled:opacity-50"
                    >
                      {updateLocation.isPending ? 'در حال ثبت...' : 'انتخاب به عنوان پیش‌فرض'}
                    </button>
                  )}
                </div>

                {/* شرح آدرس */}
                <div className="mt-3 flex flex-col gap-2 text-xs text-muted-foreground text-right leading-relaxed">
                  <p className="font-bold text-foreground">
                    {loc.province}، {loc.city}، {loc.address}
                  </p>

                  <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-x-4 gap-y-1.5 text-2xs text-muted-foreground mt-1">
                    <span>پلاک: <strong className="text-foreground">{toPersianDigits(loc.plaque)}</strong></span>
                    <span>واحد: <strong className="text-foreground">{toPersianDigits(loc.unit)}</strong></span>
                    <span>کد پستی: <strong className="text-foreground" dir="ltr">{toPersianDigits(loc.postalCode)}</strong></span>
                  </div>

                  {/* تحویل‌گیرنده */}
                  <div className="mt-2 flex items-center gap-2 border-t border-dashed border-border/60 pt-2 text-2xs">
                    <User className="h-3.5 w-3.5 text-muted-foreground" />
                    <span>تحویل‌گیرنده: </span>
                    <strong className="text-foreground">
                      {loc.isUserReceiver ? `خودتان (${loc.receiverFullName})` : loc.receiverFullName}
                    </strong>
                    {loc.receiverMobile && (
                      <span className="text-muted-foreground ms-2" dir="ltr">
                        ({toPersianDigits(loc.receiverMobile)})
                      </span>
                    )}
                  </div>
                </div>
              </div>

              {/* اکشن‌های ویرایش و حذف */}
              <div className="mt-4 flex items-center justify-end gap-2 border-t border-border/50 pt-3">
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => handleOpenEditAddressMap(loc)}
                  aria-label="ویرایش آدرس"
                  className="flex h-8 w-8 items-center justify-center rounded-lg border border-border text-muted-foreground hover:border-primary/40 hover:bg-accent hover:text-primary transition-colors"
                >
                  <Pencil className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
                </button>
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => handleDelete(loc.id)}
                  disabled={deleteLocation.isPending}
                  aria-label="حذف آدرس"
                  className="flex h-8 w-8 items-center justify-center rounded-lg border border-border text-muted-foreground hover:border-destructive/40 hover:bg-destructive/10 hover:text-destructive transition-colors"
                >
                  <Trash2 className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
                </button>
              </div>
            </Card>
          ))}
        </div>
      ) : (
        /* استیت خالی بدون آدرس */
        <div className="flex w-full flex-col items-center justify-center gap-3 rounded-2xl border border-dashed border-border/80 bg-card py-16 text-center">
          <LottiePlayer path="/animations/addresss.json" className="mx-auto h-44 w-44" />
          <span className="text-xs font-bold text-muted-foreground">
            هیچ آدرسی در حساب کاربری شما ثبت نشده است.
          </span>
          <Button
            variant="primary"
            size="sm"
            onClick={handleOpenNewAddressMap}
            className="mt-2 h-9 gap-1.5 rounded-xl px-5 text-xs font-bold"
          >
            <Plus className="h-4 w-4" />
            <span>ثبت اولین آدرس روی نقشه</span>
          </Button>
        </div>
      )}

      {/* مودال نقشه */}
      <AddressMapModal
        isOpen={isMapOpen}
        onClose={() => setIsMapOpen(false)}
        onConfirmAddress={handleConfirmMapAddress}
        initialCoordinates={mapInitialCoords}
      />

      {/* مودال تکمیل جزئیات فرم آدرس */}
      <Modal isOpen={isFormOpen} onClose={() => setIsAddFormOpen(false)} className="w-full max-w-lg overflow-hidden rounded-2xl">
        <ModalHeader onClose={() => setIsAddFormOpen(false)}>
          <ModalTitle className="flex items-center gap-2 text-sm font-black text-foreground">
            <MapPin className="h-4.5 w-4.5 text-primary" />
            <span>{editingAddress ? 'ویرایش جزئیات آدرس' : 'تکمیل جزئیات آدرس جدید'}</span>
          </ModalTitle>
        </ModalHeader>

        <ModalBody className="p-5 md:p-6 max-h-[80dvh] overflow-y-auto no-scrollbar">
          {mapData ? (
            <form onSubmit={handleSubmit(handleFormSubmit)} className="flex w-full flex-col gap-4">
              
              {/* باکس نشانی جغرافیایی انتخاب‌شده */}
              <div className="flex items-start gap-2.5 rounded-xl border border-primary/20 bg-primary/5 p-3.5 text-right">
                <Navigation className="h-4 w-4 text-primary shrink-0 mt-0.5" />
                <div className="flex flex-col gap-1 min-w-0">
                  <span className="text-3xs font-bold text-primary">موقعیت جغرافیایی انتخابی:</span>
                  <span className="text-xs font-bold text-foreground leading-relaxed">
                    {mapData.cityName}، {mapData.address}
                  </span>
                </div>
              </div>

              <Input
                label="عنوان آدرس *"
                placeholder="مثال: خانه، محل کار، تعمیرگاه"
                error={errors.title?.message}
                {...register('title')}
              />

              <Input
                label="کد پستی ۱۰ رقمی *"
                placeholder="کد پستی بدون خط تیره"
                maxLength={10}
                dir="ltr"
                error={errors.postalCode?.message}
                {...register('postalCode')}
              />

              <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-3">
                <Input
                  label="شماره پلاک *"
                  placeholder="پلاک..."
                  error={errors.plaque?.message}
                  {...register('plaque')}
                />
                <Input
                  label="شماره واحد *"
                  placeholder="واحد..."
                  error={errors.unit?.message}
                  {...register('unit')}
                />
              </div>

              {/* گزینه تحویل گیرنده */}
              <div className="flex items-center gap-2.5 pt-1 select-none">
                <input
                  type="checkbox"
                  id="user_is_receiver"
                  className="h-4 w-4 rounded-md border-border text-primary focus:ring-primary cursor-pointer"
                  {...register('isUserReceiver')}
                />
                <label htmlFor="user_is_receiver" className="text-xs font-bold text-foreground cursor-pointer">
                  تحویل‌گیرنده سفارش خودم هستم
                </label>
              </div>

              {!watchIsUserReceiver && (
                <div className="flex flex-col gap-3 rounded-xl border border-dashed border-border/80 bg-muted/20 p-3.5 mt-1">
                  <span className="text-3xs font-bold text-muted-foreground">مشخصات شخص تحویل‌گیرنده:</span>
                  <Input
                    label="نام و نام خانوادگی گیرنده *"
                    placeholder="نام کامل تحویل‌گیرنده"
                    error={errors.receiverFullName?.message}
                    {...register('receiverFullName')}
                  />
                  <Input
                    label="شماره موبایل گیرنده *"
                    placeholder="0912..."
                    maxLength={11}
                    dir="ltr"
                    error={errors.receiverMobile?.message}
                    {...register('receiverMobile')}
                  />
                </div>
              )}

              {/* دکمه‌های پایانی فرم */}
              <div className="mt-3 flex w-full items-center gap-2.5 pt-3 border-t border-border/60">
                <Button
                  type="button"
                  variant="outline"
                  onClick={() => setIsAddFormOpen(false)}
                  className="h-10 flex-1 rounded-xl text-xs font-bold"
                >
                  انصراف
                </Button>
                <Button
                  type="submit"
                  variant="primary"
                  isLoading={createLocation.isPending || updateLocation.isPending}
                  className="h-10 flex-1 rounded-xl text-xs font-bold shadow-xs"
                >
                  {editingAddress ? 'ذخیره تغییرات' : 'ثبت نهایی آدرس'}
                </Button>
              </div>
            </form>
          ) : null}
        </ModalBody>
      </Modal>

    </div>
  );
}

'use client';

import * as React from 'react';
import Link from 'next/link';
import { useRouter } from 'next/navigation';
import { useQueryClient } from '@tanstack/react-query';
import {
  MessageSquare,
  ArrowRight,
  Star,
  Clock,
  Trash2,
  Pencil,
  Plus,
  Search,
  BookOpen,
} from 'lucide-react';
import {
  useGetPendingComments,
  useGetUserComments,
  useDeleteComment,
} from '@/domains/front/comment/hooks/comment.hooks';
import { useGetUserInquiries, useDeleteInquiry } from '@/domains/front/inquiry/hooks/inquiry.hooks';
import { useGetUserBlogPostComments } from '@/domains/blog/hooks/blog.hooks';
import { Card } from '@/components/composites/Card';
import { Button } from '@/components/primitives/Button/Button';
import { PageLoading } from '@/components/composites/Loading/PageLoading';
import { Pagination } from '@/components/composites/Pagination/Pagination';
import { LottiePlayer } from '@/components/shared/Lottie/LottiePlayer';
import { WriteCommentModal } from './components/WriteCommentModal';
import { showToast } from '@/core/utils/toast';
import { cn } from '@/design-system/utils/cn';
import { getFullUrl } from '@/core/utils/formatters';

const TABS = [
  { key: 'pending' as const, label: 'در انتظار نظر', icon: Clock },
  { key: 'comments' as const, label: 'نظرات محصولات', icon: MessageSquare },
  { key: 'inquiries' as const, label: 'پرسش‌ها', icon: Search },
  { key: 'blogComments' as const, label: 'نظرات بلاگ', icon: BookOpen },
] as const;

type TabKey = (typeof TABS)[number]['key'];

interface EmptyStateProps {
  title: string;
  description: string;
  actionText?: string;
  actionHref?: string;
}

function EmptyState({ title, description, actionText, actionHref }: EmptyStateProps) {
  const router = useRouter();

  return (
    <div className="flex w-full flex-col items-center justify-center rounded-2xl border border-dashed border-border/80 bg-card py-12 text-center shadow-xs">
      <div className="mx-auto flex h-48 w-48 max-w-full items-center justify-center -mb-2">
        <LottiePlayer
          path="/anumations/customer-service-chat.json"
          className="h-full w-full"
          loop={true}
        />
      </div>
      <p className="mt-5 text-sm font-bold text-foreground">{title}</p>
      <p className="mt-1 text-xs text-muted-foreground">{description}</p>
      {/* {actionText && actionHref && (
        <Button
          variant="outline"
          size="sm"
          onClick={() => router.push(actionHref)}
          className="mt-4 h-9 gap-1.5 rounded-xl px-5 text-xs font-bold shadow-xs"
        >
          <Plus className="h-4 w-4" />
          <span>{actionText}</span>
        </Button>
      )} */}
    </div>
  );
}

export function CommentsDashboard() {
  const router = useRouter();
  const queryClient = useQueryClient();

  const [activeTab, setActiveTab] = React.useState<TabKey>('pending');
  const [pendingPage, setPendingPage] = React.useState(1);
  const [commentsPage, setCommentsPage] = React.useState(1);
  const [inquiriesPage, setInquiriesPage] = React.useState(1);
  const [blogCommentsPage, setBlogCommentsPage] = React.useState(1);

  const [selectedProduct, setSelectedProduct] = React.useState<any | null>(null);
  const [editingComment, setEditingComment] = React.useState<any | null>(null);
  const [isModalOpen, setIsModalOpen] = React.useState(false);

  const { data: pendingResponse, isLoading: isPendingLoading } = useGetPendingComments(pendingPage, 10);
  const { data: commentsResponse, isLoading: isCommentsLoading } = useGetUserComments(commentsPage, 10);
  const { data: inquiriesResponse, isLoading: isInquiriesLoading } = useGetUserInquiries(inquiriesPage, 10);
  const { data: blogCommentsResponse, isLoading: isBlogCommentsLoading } = useGetUserBlogPostComments(blogCommentsPage, 10);

  const deleteComment = useDeleteComment();
  const deleteInquiry = useDeleteInquiry();

  const pendingItems = pendingResponse?.items || [];
  const pendingTotalPages = pendingResponse?.totalPages || 1;
  const pendingCount = pendingResponse?.totalCount || 0;

  const commentItems = commentsResponse?.items || [];
  const commentsTotalPages = commentsResponse?.totalPages || 1;
  const commentsCount = commentsResponse?.totalCount || 0;

  const inquiryItems = inquiriesResponse?.items || [];
  const inquiriesTotalPages = inquiriesResponse?.totalPages || 1;
  const inquiriesCount = inquiriesResponse?.totalCount || 0;

  const blogCommentItems = blogCommentsResponse?.items || [];
  const blogCommentsTotalPages = blogCommentsResponse?.totalPages || 1;
  const blogCommentsCount = blogCommentsResponse?.totalCount || 0;

  const counts: Record<TabKey, number> = {
    pending: pendingCount,
    comments: commentsCount,
    inquiries: inquiriesCount,
    blogComments: blogCommentsCount,
  };

  const handleOpenWriteComment = (product: any) => {
    setSelectedProduct(product);
    setEditingComment(null);
    setIsModalOpen(true);
  };

  const handleOpenEditComment = (comment: any) => {
    setSelectedProduct({
      productId: comment.productId,
      productTitle: comment.productTitle,
      productImage: comment.productImage,
    });
    setEditingComment(comment);
    setIsModalOpen(true);
  };

  const handleDeleteComment = async (id: string) => {
    try {
      await deleteComment.mutateAsync(id);
      showToast.success('نظر شما با موفقیت حذف شد');
      queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ['user', 'comments'] });
    } catch {}
  };

  const handleDeleteInquiry = async (id: string) => {
    try {
      await deleteInquiry.mutateAsync(id);
      showToast.success('پرسش شما با موفقیت حذف شد');
      queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ['user', 'inquiries'] });
    } catch {}
  };

  return (
    <div className="flex w-full flex-1 flex-col gap-6 select-none" dir="rtl">
      <div className="flex w-full flex-col gap-2 border-b border-border pb-5">
        <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
          <MessageSquare aria-hidden="true" className="h-5 w-5 shrink-0 text-primary" />
          <span className="text-xl font-bold text-foreground">نظرات و پرسش‌ها</span>
        </div>
        <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">
          مدیریت نظرات محصولات، پرسش‌های ثبت‌شده در بخش پرسش و پاسخ، و نظرات وبلاگ
        </p>
      </div>

      {/* تب‌ها */}
      <div className="flex w-full shrink-0 select-none items-center gap-4 overflow-x-auto px-1 py-2 scrollbar-none">
        {TABS.map((tab) => {
          const isActive = activeTab === tab.key;
          const TabIcon = tab.icon;
          return (
            <button
              key={tab.key}
              type="button"
              onClick={() => setActiveTab(tab.key)}
              aria-pressed={isActive}
              className={cn(
                'flex h-24 w-32 shrink-0 flex-col items-center justify-center gap-1.5 rounded-lg border bg-card px-3 text-center outline-none shadow-xs transition-colors duration-fast md:h-24 md:w-36',
                isActive
                  ? 'border-primary bg-accent font-bold text-accent-foreground'
                  : 'border-border text-muted-foreground hover:border-primary/40 hover:text-foreground'
              )}
            >
              <TabIcon
                aria-hidden="true"
                className={cn('h-5 w-5 shrink-0', isActive ? 'text-primary' : 'text-muted-foreground')}
              />
              <span className="text-xs font-bold">{tab.label}</span>
              <span
                className={cn(
                  'mt-0.5 rounded-full px-2 py-0.5 text-2xs font-bold',
                  isActive ? 'bg-primary/15 text-primary' : 'bg-muted text-muted-foreground'
                )}
              >
                {counts[tab.key]}
              </span>
            </button>
          );
        })}
      </div>

      {/* تب در انتظار نظر */}
      {activeTab === 'pending' ? (
        <div className="flex w-full flex-col gap-5">
          {isPendingLoading ? (
            <PageLoading label="در حال بارگذاری کالاها..." />
          ) : pendingItems.length > 0 ? (
            <div className="flex w-full flex-col gap-4">
              <div className="grid w-full grid-cols-1 gap-4 md:grid-cols-2">
                {pendingItems.map((item: any) => (
                  <Card
                    key={item.productId}
                    className="flex w-full flex-col items-stretch justify-between gap-4 p-5 transition-colors hover:border-primary/40 sm:flex-row sm:items-center"
                  >
                    <div className="flex min-w-0 flex-1 items-center gap-3.5">
                      <div className="flex h-16 w-16 shrink-0 items-center justify-center overflow-hidden rounded-md border border-border bg-muted p-0.5">
                        <img src={getFullUrl(item.productImage)} className="h-full w-full object-contain" alt="" />
                      </div>
                      <div className="flex min-w-0 flex-1 flex-col gap-1">
                        <span className="block truncate text-sm font-bold text-foreground">{item.productTitle}</span>
                        <span className="block text-2xs text-muted-foreground">
                          خریداری شده از فروشگاه {item.shopTitle}
                        </span>
                        <span className="mt-1 block text-2xs text-muted-foreground/80">
                          تاریخ خرید: {item.lastSaleDateFormatted}
                        </span>
                      </div>
                    </div>

                    <Button
                      variant="primary"
                      size="sm"
                      onClick={() => handleOpenWriteComment(item)}
                      className="h-9 shrink-0 px-4 text-2xs font-bold shadow-xs"
                    >
                      <Plus aria-hidden="true" className="h-4 w-4" />
                      <span>ثبت نظر و امتیاز</span>
                    </Button>
                  </Card>
                ))}
              </div>

              <Pagination
                currentPage={pendingPage}
                totalPages={pendingTotalPages}
                onPageChange={(p) => setPendingPage(p)}
              />
            </div>
          ) : (
            <EmptyState
              title="کالایی در انتظار نظر شما وجود ندارد"
              description="تمامی کالاهای خریداری‌شده توسط شما بررسی و ثبت نظر شده‌اند."
              actionText="مشاهده سفارش‌ها"
              actionHref="/profile/orders"
            />
          )}
        </div>
      ) : null}

      {/* تب نظرات محصولات */}
      {activeTab === 'comments' ? (
        <div className="flex w-full flex-col gap-5">
          {isCommentsLoading ? (
            <PageLoading label="در حال بارگذاری نظرات..." />
          ) : commentItems.length > 0 ? (
            <div className="flex w-full flex-col gap-4">
              <div className="grid w-full grid-cols-1 gap-4 md:grid-cols-2">
                {commentItems.map((comment: any) => (
                  <Card key={comment.id} className="flex w-full flex-col justify-between gap-4 p-5">
                    <div className="flex w-full items-center gap-3.5 border-b border-border pb-3">
                      <div className="flex h-10 w-10 shrink-0 items-center justify-center overflow-hidden rounded-md border border-border bg-muted p-0.5">
                        <img src={getFullUrl(comment.productImage)} className="h-full w-full object-contain" alt="" />
                      </div>
                      <div className="min-w-0 flex-1">
                        <span className="block truncate text-xs font-bold text-foreground">{comment.productTitle}</span>
                        <div className="mt-1 flex items-center gap-1.5">
                          <span
                            className={cn(
                              'rounded-full px-2 py-0.5 text-2xs font-bold',
                              comment.isConfirmed ? 'bg-success/10 text-success' : 'bg-warning/10 text-warning'
                            )}
                          >
                            {comment.isConfirmed ? 'تایید شده' : 'در حال بررسی'}
                          </span>
                          <span className="text-2xs text-muted-foreground/80">{comment.createDateFormatted}</span>
                        </div>
                      </div>
                    </div>

                    <div className="flex flex-col gap-1.5">
                      <div className="flex items-center gap-0.5">
                        {Array.from({ length: 5 }).map((_, i) => (
                          <Star
                            key={i}
                            aria-hidden="true"
                            className={cn(
                              'h-3.5 w-3.5 shrink-0',
                              i < comment.rate ? 'fill-warning text-warning' : 'text-border'
                            )}
                          />
                        ))}
                      </div>
                      <p className="mt-1 break-words text-sm leading-relaxed text-foreground">{comment.comment}</p>
                    </div>

                    <div className="mt-1.5 flex items-center justify-end gap-2.5 border-t border-dashed border-border pt-3">
                      <button
                        type="button"
                        onClick={() => handleOpenEditComment(comment)}
                        aria-label="ویرایش"
                        className="rounded-md border border-border p-1.5 text-muted-foreground transition-colors hover:border-primary/40 hover:bg-accent hover:text-primary"
                      >
                        <Pencil aria-hidden="true" className="h-4 w-4" />
                      </button>
                      <button
                        type="button"
                        onClick={() => handleDeleteComment(comment.id)}
                        disabled={deleteComment.isPending}
                        aria-label="حذف"
                        className="rounded-md border border-border p-1.5 text-muted-foreground transition-colors hover:border-destructive/40 hover:bg-destructive/10 hover:text-destructive"
                      >
                        <Trash2 aria-hidden="true" className="h-4 w-4" />
                      </button>
                    </div>
                  </Card>
                ))}
              </div>

              <Pagination
                currentPage={commentsPage}
                totalPages={commentsTotalPages}
                onPageChange={(p) => setCommentsPage(p)}
              />
            </div>
          ) : (
            <EmptyState
              title="هنوز نظری ثبت نکرده‌اید"
              description="نظرات و بازخوردهای شما درباره محصولات در این بخش نمایش داده می‌شوند."
              actionText="مشاهده سفارش‌ها و ثبت نظر"
              actionHref="/profile/orders"
            />
          )}
        </div>
      ) : null}

      {/* تب پرسش‌ها */}
      {activeTab === 'inquiries' ? (
        <div className="flex w-full flex-col gap-5">
          {isInquiriesLoading ? (
            <PageLoading label="در حال بارگذاری پرسش‌ها..." />
          ) : inquiryItems.length > 0 ? (
            <div className="flex w-full flex-col gap-4">
              <div className="grid w-full grid-cols-1 gap-4 md:grid-cols-2">
                {inquiryItems.map((inquiry: any) => (
                  <Card key={inquiry.id} className="flex w-full flex-col justify-between gap-4 p-5">
                    <div className="flex w-full items-center gap-3.5 border-b border-border pb-3">
                      <div className="flex h-10 w-10 shrink-0 items-center justify-center overflow-hidden rounded-md border border-border bg-muted p-0.5">
                        <img src={getFullUrl(inquiry.productImage)} className="h-full w-full object-contain" alt="" />
                      </div>
                      <div className="min-w-0 flex-1">
                        <span className="block truncate text-xs font-bold text-foreground">{inquiry.productTitle}</span>
                        <div className="mt-1 flex items-center gap-1.5">
                          <span
                            className={cn(
                              'rounded-full px-2 py-0.5 text-2xs font-bold',
                              inquiry.isConfirmed ? 'bg-success/10 text-success' : 'bg-warning/10 text-warning'
                            )}
                          >
                            {inquiry.isConfirmed ? 'تایید شده' : 'در حال بررسی'}
                          </span>
                          <span className="text-2xs text-muted-foreground/80">{inquiry.createDateFormatted}</span>
                        </div>
                      </div>
                    </div>

                    <div className="flex flex-col gap-1.5">
                      <span className="text-2xs font-bold text-primary">متن پرسش:</span>
                      <p className="break-words text-sm leading-relaxed text-foreground">{inquiry.comment}</p>
                      <span className="mt-1.5 text-2xs font-bold text-muted-foreground">
                        {inquiry.replyCount} پاسخ ثبت شده
                      </span>
                    </div>

                    <div className="mt-1.5 flex items-center justify-end border-t border-dashed border-border pt-3">
                      <button
                        type="button"
                        onClick={() => handleDeleteInquiry(inquiry.id)}
                        disabled={deleteInquiry.isPending}
                        aria-label="حذف"
                        className="rounded-md border border-border p-1.5 text-muted-foreground transition-colors hover:border-destructive/40 hover:bg-destructive/10 hover:text-destructive"
                      >
                        <Trash2 aria-hidden="true" className="h-4 w-4" />
                      </button>
                    </div>
                  </Card>
                ))}
              </div>

              <Pagination
                currentPage={inquiriesPage}
                totalPages={inquiriesTotalPages}
                onPageChange={(p) => setInquiriesPage(p)}
              />
            </div>
          ) : (
            <EmptyState
              title="هنوز پرسشی ثبت نکرده‌اید"
              description="پرسش‌های مطرح‌شده توسط شما درباره کالاها در این بخش قابل مشاهده و پیگیری است."
              actionText="مشاهده محصولات"
              actionHref="/search"
            />
          )}
        </div>
      ) : null}

      {/* تب نظرات بلاگ */}
      {activeTab === 'blogComments' ? (
        <div className="flex w-full flex-col gap-5">
          {isBlogCommentsLoading ? (
            <PageLoading label="در حال بارگذاری نظرات بلاگ..." />
          ) : blogCommentItems.length > 0 ? (
            <div className="flex w-full flex-col gap-4">
              <div className="grid w-full grid-cols-1 gap-4 md:grid-cols-2">
                {blogCommentItems.map((comment: any) => (
                  <Card key={comment.id} className="flex w-full flex-col justify-between gap-4 p-5">
                    <div className="flex w-full items-center gap-3.5 border-b border-border pb-3">
                      <div className="flex h-10 w-10 shrink-0 items-center justify-center overflow-hidden rounded-md border border-accent bg-accent p-0.5 text-primary">
                        <BookOpen aria-hidden="true" className="h-5 w-5" />
                      </div>
                      <div className="min-w-0 flex-1">
                        <Link
                          href={comment.blogPostEnglishTitle ? `/blog/${encodeURIComponent(comment.blogPostEnglishTitle)}` : '/blog'}
                          className="block truncate text-xs font-bold text-foreground transition-colors hover:text-primary"
                        >
                          {comment.blogPostTitle}
                        </Link>
                        <div className="mt-1 flex items-center gap-1.5">
                          <span
                            className={cn(
                              'rounded-full px-2 py-0.5 text-2xs font-bold',
                              comment.isConfirmed ? 'bg-success/10 text-success' : 'bg-warning/10 text-warning'
                            )}
                          >
                            {comment.isConfirmed ? 'تایید شده' : 'در حال بررسی'}
                          </span>
                          <span className="text-2xs text-muted-foreground/80">{comment.createDateFormatted}</span>
                        </div>
                      </div>
                    </div>

                    <div className="flex flex-col gap-1.5">
                      <span className="text-2xs font-bold text-primary">متن دیدگاه:</span>
                      <p className="break-words text-sm leading-relaxed text-foreground">{comment.comment}</p>
                    </div>

                    <div className="mt-1.5 flex items-center justify-end border-t border-dashed border-border pt-3">
                      <Link
                        href={comment.blogPostEnglishTitle ? `/blog/${encodeURIComponent(comment.blogPostEnglishTitle)}` : '/blog'}
                        className="flex items-center gap-1 text-xs font-bold text-primary hover:underline"
                      >
                        <span>مشاهده مقاله</span>
                        <ArrowRight aria-hidden="true" className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
                      </Link>
                    </div>
                  </Card>
                ))}
              </div>

              <Pagination
                currentPage={blogCommentsPage}
                totalPages={blogCommentsTotalPages}
                onPageChange={(p) => setBlogCommentsPage(p)}
              />
            </div>
          ) : (
            <EmptyState
              title="هنوز دیدگاهی برای مقالات ثبت نکرده‌اید"
              description="نظرات شما در بخش مقالات و وبلاگ پس از ثبت در این قسمت نمایش داده می‌شوند."
              actionText="مشاهده مقالات بلاگ"
              actionHref="/blog"
            />
          )}
        </div>
      ) : null}

      <WriteCommentModal
        isOpen={isModalOpen}
        onClose={() => setIsModalOpen(false)}
        product={selectedProduct}
        editingComment={editingComment}
      />
    </div>
  );
}

export function CreateTicket() {

      const filesArray = Array.from(e.target.files);

'use client';

import * as React from 'react';
import { useRouter } from 'next/navigation';
import { Heart, ArrowRight, ShoppingBag } from 'lucide-react';
import { useGetFavoriteProducts } from '@/domains/userpanel/hooks/userpanel.hooks';
import { ProductSearchCard } from '@/components/features/ProductCard/ProductSearchCard';
import { PageLoading } from '@/components/composites/Loading/PageLoading';
import { Pagination } from '@/components/composites/Pagination/Pagination';
import { Select } from '@/components/primitives/Select/Select';
import { Button } from '@/components/primitives/Button/Button';

function LottiePlayer({ path, className = 'w-48 h-48', loop = true }: { path: string; className?: string; loop?: boolean }) {
  const containerRef = React.useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);

  React.useEffect(() => {
    let anim: any = null;
    let isMounted = true;

    const init = async () => {
      try {
        const altPath = path.includes('/anumations/')
          ? path.replace('/anumations/', '/animations/')
          : path.replace('/animations/', '/anumations/');
        let animationData: any = null;
        for (const p of [path, altPath]) {
          try {
            const res = await fetch(p);
            if (res.ok) {
              animationData = await res.json();
              break;
            }
          } catch {}
        }
        if (!animationData || !isMounted || !containerRef.current) return;
        const lottieModule = await import('lottie-web');
        const lottie = lottieModule.default || lottieModule;
        if (containerRef.current && isMounted) {
          anim = lottie.loadAnimation({
            container: containerRef.current,
            renderer: 'svg',
            loop,
            autoplay: true,
            animationData,
          });
        }
      } catch {}
    };

    init();
    return () => {
      isMounted = false;
      anim?.destroy();
    };
  }, [path, loop]);

  return <div ref={containerRef} className={className} />;
}

const ORDER_OPTIONS = [
  { value: 'Latest', label: 'جدیدترین‌ها' },
  { value: 'Oldest', label: 'قدیمی‌ترین‌ها' },
  { value: 'HighestRate', label: 'بیشترین امتیاز' },
  { value: 'LowestRate', label: 'کمترین امتیاز' },
  { value: 'HighestPrice', label: 'بیشترین قیمت' },
  { value: 'LowestPrice', label: 'کمترین قیمت' },
] as const;

export function FavoritesList() {
  const router = useRouter();
  const [order, setOrder] = React.useState('Latest');
  const [page, setPage] = React.useState(1);

  const { data: favoritesResponse, isLoading } = useGetFavoriteProducts(page, order);
  const favorites = favoritesResponse?.items || [];
  const totalPages = favoritesResponse?.totalPages || 1;

  return (
    <div className="flex w-full flex-1 flex-col gap-6">
      <div className="mb-1 flex items-center justify-between border-b border-border pb-3 lg:hidden">
        <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => router.push('/profile')}
            aria-label="بازگشت"
            className="-me-1 flex items-center justify-center rounded-full p-1 transition-colors hover:bg-muted"
          >
            <ArrowRight aria-hidden="true" className="h-5 w-5 text-foreground" />
          </button>
          <span className="text-sm font-bold text-foreground">کالاهای محبوب من</span>
        </div>
      </div>

      <div className="flex w-full flex-col justify-between gap-4 border-b border-border pb-5 sm:flex-row sm:items-center">
        <div className="flex flex-col gap-2">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
            <Heart aria-hidden="true" className="h-5 w-5 shrink-0 text-primary" />
            <span className="text-xl font-bold text-foreground">کالاهای محبوب</span>
          </div>
          <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">
            لیست کالاهایی که با قلبی کردن در صفحات محصول، برای دسترسی سریع ذخیره کرده‌اید
          </p>
        </div>

        <div className="w-full shrink-0 select-none sm:max-w-xs">
          <Select
            placeholder="مرتب‌سازی بر اساس"
            value={order}
            onChange={(e) => {
              setOrder(e.target.value);
              setPage(1);
            }}
            options={[...ORDER_OPTIONS]}
          />
        </div>
      </div>

      {isLoading ? (
        <PageLoading label="در حال دریافت کالاهای محبوب شما..." />
      ) : favorites.length > 0 ? (
        <div className="flex w-full flex-col gap-6">
          <div className="grid w-full grid-cols-1 md:gap-3 sm:grid-cols-2 md:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-3 xl:grid-cols-4 2xl:grid-cols-5">
            {favorites.map((product: any) => (
              <ProductSearchCard key={product.id} product={product} />
            ))}
          </div>

          <Pagination currentPage={page} totalPages={totalPages} onPageChange={(p) => setPage(p)} />
        </div>
      ) : (
        <div className="flex w-full flex-col items-center justify-center gap-3 rounded-lg border border-dashed border-border bg-card px-4 py-12 text-center">
          <LottiePlayer path="/anumations/Like.json" className="mx-auto h-48 w-48 max-w-full" />
          <span className="text-sm font-bold text-muted-foreground">
            هنوز هیچ کالایی را به لیست محبوب‌های خود اضافه نکرده‌اید.
          </span>
          <Button
            variant="outline"
            size="sm"
            onClick={() => router.push('/search')}
            className="mt-2 flex h-10 items-center justify-center gap-1.5 px-6 py-2 text-xs font-bold shadow-xs"
          >
            <ShoppingBag aria-hidden="true" className="h-4 w-4" />
            <span>مشاهده و خرید کالاها</span>
          </Button>
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

function getChannelMeta(channel: string) {
  switch (channel) {
    case 'Discount':
      return {
        icon: <Tag className="h-4.5 w-4.5 text-primary" />,
        bg: 'bg-primary/10 border-primary/20',
        badgeLabel: 'تخفیف و پیشنهاد',
      };
    case 'System':
      return {
        icon: <Settings className="h-4.5 w-4.5 text-muted-foreground" />,
        bg: 'bg-muted border-border',
        badgeLabel: 'سیستمی',
      };
    case 'Information':
      return {
        icon: <Info className="h-4.5 w-4.5 text-blue-500" />,
        bg: 'bg-blue-500/10 border-blue-500/20',
        badgeLabel: 'اطلاعیه',
      };
    case 'AdminMessage':
      return {
        icon: <ShieldAlert className="h-4.5 w-4.5 text-destructive" />,
        bg: 'bg-destructive/10 border-destructive/20',
        badgeLabel: 'پیام مدیریت',
      };
    case 'SellerMessage':
      return {
        icon: <Store className="h-4.5 w-4.5 text-amber-500" />,
        bg: 'bg-amber-500/10 border-amber-500/20',
        badgeLabel: 'پیام فروشنده',
      };
    case 'Advertise':
      return {
        icon: <Sparkles className="h-4.5 w-4.5 text-amber-500" />,
        bg: 'bg-amber-500/10 border-amber-500/20',
        badgeLabel: 'ویژه',
      };
    case 'WebBroadcastMessage':
      return {
        icon: <Radio className="h-4.5 w-4.5 text-primary" />,
        bg: 'bg-primary/10 border-primary/20',
        badgeLabel: 'اطلاع‌رسانی عمومی',
      };
    default:
      return {
        icon: <Bell className="h-4.5 w-4.5 text-muted-foreground" />,
        bg: 'bg-muted border-border',
        badgeLabel: 'پیام',
      };
  }
}

      <div className="flex flex-col gap-3 rounded-2xl border border-border/80 bg-card p-3 shadow-2xs sm:flex-row sm:items-center sm:justify-between select-none">
        
        {/* تب‌های کپسولی */}
        <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 rounded-xl bg-muted/60 p-1">
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => handleTabChange('all')}
            className={cn(
              'flex items-center gap-2 rounded-lg px-3 py-1.5 text-xs font-bold transition-all',
              activeTab === 'all'
                ? 'bg-card text-foreground shadow-xs'
                : 'text-muted-foreground hover:text-foreground'
            )}
          >
            <span>همه پیام‌ها</span>
            <span
              className={cn(
                'rounded-md px-1.5 py-0.2 text-3xs  font-bold',
                activeTab === 'all' ? 'bg-primary/10 text-primary' : 'bg-muted text-muted-foreground'
              )}
            >
              {toPersianDigits(totalCount)}
            </span>
          </button>

          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => handleTabChange('discounts')}
            className={cn(
              'flex items-center gap-2 rounded-lg px-3 py-1.5 text-xs font-bold transition-all',
              activeTab === 'discounts'
                ? 'bg-card text-foreground shadow-xs'
                : 'text-muted-foreground hover:text-foreground'
            )}
          >
            <span>تخفیف‌ها</span>
            <span
              className={cn(
                'rounded-md px-1.5 py-0.2 text-3xs  font-bold',
                activeTab === 'discounts' ? 'bg-primary/10 text-primary' : 'bg-muted text-muted-foreground'
              )}
            >
              {toPersianDigits(discountCount)}
            </span>
          </button>
        </div>

        {/* فیلترهای اولویت و ترتیب (در موبایل هر کدام ۵۰٪ عرض می‌گیرند) */}
        <div className="flex items-center gap-2 w-full sm:w-auto">
          <div className="flex-1 sm:w-36">
            <Select
              placeholder="اولویت"
              value={priority}
              onChange={(e) => {
                setPriority(e.target.value);
                setPage(1);
              }}
              options={[...PRIORITY_OPTIONS]}
            />
          </div>

          <div className="flex-1 sm:w-36">
            <Select
              placeholder="مرتب‌سازی"
              value={orderBy}
              onChange={(e) => {
                setOrderBy(e.target.value as any);
                setPage(1);
              }}
              options={[...ORDER_OPTIONS]}
            />
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* لیست اعلان‌ها */}
      {isLoading ? (
        <PageLoading label="در حال دریافت پیام‌های شما..." />
      ) : notifications.length > 0 ? (
        <div className="flex flex-col gap-3 sm:gap-4">
          {notifications.map((notif: any) => {
            const meta = getChannelMeta(notif.channel);

            return (
              <Card
                key={notif.id}
                className={cn(
                  'relative overflow-hidden rounded-2xl border p-4 sm:p-5 transition-all shadow-xs',
                  !notif.isRead
                    ? 'border-primary/40 bg-gradient-to-r from-primary/5 via-card to-card'
                    : 'border-border/80 bg-card hover:border-border'
                )}
              >
                {/* نشانگر نقطه نورانی برای پیام‌های خوانده‌نشده */}
                {!notif.isRead && (
                  <span className="absolute top-4 start-4 flex h-2 w-2">
                    <span className="animate-ping absolute inline-flex h-full w-full rounded-full bg-primary opacity-75" />
                    <span className="relative inline-flex rounded-full h-2 w-2 bg-primary" />
                  </span>
                )}

                <div className="flex items-start gap-3.5">
                  {/* آیکون کانال با پدینگ و فرم زیبا */}
                  <div className={cn('flex h-11 w-11 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-2xl border shadow-2xs', meta.bg)}>
                    {meta.icon}
                  </div>

                  <div className="flex flex-1 flex-col gap-1.5 min-w-0">
                    {/* ردیف تایتل و تگ نوع پیام */}
                    <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-2">
                      <div className="flex items-center gap-2 min-w-0">
                        <span className="truncate text-xs sm:text-sm font-black text-foreground">
                          {notif.title}
                        </span>
                        <span className="hidden sm:inline-block rounded-md bg-muted px-2 py-0.5 text-3xs font-bold text-muted-foreground">
                          {meta.badgeLabel}
                        </span>
                      </div>

                      <div className="flex items-center gap-1 text-3xs text-muted-foreground font-medium shrink-0">
                        <Clock className="h-3 w-3 text-muted-foreground/80" />
                        <span>{notif.createDateFormatted}</span>
                      </div>
                    </div>

                    {/* متن پیام با فاصله‌گذاری و خوانایی بالا */}
                    <p className="mt-0.5 text-xs text-muted-foreground leading-relaxed text-right whitespace-pre-line break-words">
                      {notif.body}
                    </p>

                    {/* دکمه اکشن در صورت وجود لینک اختصاصی */}
                    {notif.linkType && notif.linkText && (
                      <div className="mt-3 flex justify-start">
                        <Button
                          variant="outline"
                          size="sm"
                          onClick={() => router.push(notif.linkType as string)}
                          className="h-8 gap-1.5 rounded-xl border-primary/30 px-3.5 text-2xs font-bold text-primary hover:bg-primary/5 active:scale-95 transition-all"
                        >
                          <span>{notif.linkText}</span>
                          <ChevronLeft className="h-3 w-3" />
                        </Button>
                      </div>
                    )}
                  </div>
                </div>
              </Card>
            );
          })}

          <Pagination
            currentPage={page}
            totalPages={totalPages}
            onPageChange={(p) => setPage(p)}
          />
        </div>
      ) : (
        /* استیت خالی اعلان‌ها */
        <div className="flex w-full flex-col items-center justify-center gap-3 rounded-2xl border border-dashed border-border/80 bg-card py-16 text-center">
          <LottiePlayer path="/animations/notif-bell.json" className="mx-auto h-40 w-40" />
          <span className="text-xs font-bold text-muted-foreground">
            هیچ پیامی در این دسته‌بندی یافت نشد.
          </span>
        </div>
      )}

    </div>
  );
}
