'use client';

import * as React from 'react';
import { useRouter } from 'next/navigation';
import {
  ShoppingBag,
  CreditCard,
  Truck,
  CheckCircle2,
  RotateCcw,
  XCircle,
  ChevronLeft,
  Clock,
  ImageIcon,
  Calendar,
  Plus,
} from 'lucide-react';
import { useGetOrders } from '@/domains/userpanel/hooks/userpanel.hooks';
import { Card } from '@/components/composites/Card';
import { Button } from '@/components/primitives/Button/Button';
import { PageLoading } from '@/components/composites/Loading/PageLoading';
import { Pagination } from '@/components/composites/Pagination/Pagination';
import { LottiePlayer } from '@/components/shared/Lottie/LottiePlayer';
import { cn } from '@/design-system/utils/cn';
import { getFullUrl, toPersianDigits } from '@/core/utils/formatters';

interface PayableTimerProps {
  payableUntil: string;
}

function PayableTimer({ payableUntil }: PayableTimerProps) {
  const [timeLeft, setTimeLeft] = React.useState('');

  React.useEffect(() => {
    const calculateTime = () => {
      const diff = new Date(payableUntil).getTime() - Date.now();
      if (diff > 0) {
        const mins = Math.floor(diff / 1000 / 60);
        const secs = Math.floor((diff / 1000) % 60);
        const formattedMins = String(mins).padStart(2, '0');
        const formattedSecs = String(secs).padStart(2, '0');
        setTimeLeft(`${toPersianDigits(formattedMins)}:${toPersianDigits(formattedSecs)}`);
      } else {
        setTimeLeft('منقضی شده');
      }
    };
    calculateTime();
    const interval = setInterval(calculateTime, 1000);
    return () => clearInterval(interval);
  }, [payableUntil]);

  const expired = timeLeft === 'منقضی شده';

  return (
    <div
      className={cn(
        'flex items-center gap-1.5 text-xs font-bold',
        expired ? 'text-muted-foreground' : 'text-destructive'
      )}
      dir="ltr"
    >
      <span>{timeLeft}</span>
      {!expired ? <Clock className="h-3.5 w-3.5 animate-spin" style={{ animationDuration: '4s' }} /> : null}
    </div>
  );
}

// تابع امن و دقیق فرمت قیمت بدون تقسیم اضافی
const formatOrderPrice = (val: number) => {
  if (val === null || val === undefined || isNaN(val)) return '۰ تومان';
  return new Intl.NumberFormat('fa-IR').format(val) + ' تومان';
};

const STATUS_TABS = [
  { id: '', label: 'همه سفارش‌ها', icon: ShoppingBag },
  { id: 'WaitingForPayment', label: 'در انتظار پرداخت', icon: CreditCard },
  { id: 'InProgress', label: 'در حال پردازش', icon: Truck },
  { id: 'Delivered', label: 'تحویل شده', icon: CheckCircle2 },
  { id: 'ReturnRequest', label: 'مرجوعی', icon: RotateCcw },
  { id: 'Cancelled', label: 'لغو شده', icon: XCircle },
] as const;

export interface OrdersListProps {
  initialStatus: string;
  initialPage: number;
}

export function OrdersList({ initialStatus, initialPage }: OrdersListProps) {
  const router = useRouter();
  const [status, setStatus] = React.useState(initialStatus);
  const [page, setPage] = React.useState(initialPage);

  const { data: ordersResponse, isLoading } = useGetOrders(status, '', '', page);
  const orders = ordersResponse?.items || [];
  const totalPages = ordersResponse?.totalPages || 1;

  const handleTabChange = (newStatus: string) => {
    setStatus(newStatus);
    setPage(1);
    router.push(`/profile/orders?status=${newStatus}`);
  };

  const getOrderStatusMeta = (orderStatus: string) => {
    switch (orderStatus) {
      case 'WaitingForPayment':
        return { label: 'در انتظار پرداخت', color: 'text-amber-500 bg-amber-500/10 border-amber-500/20' };
      case 'InProgress':
        return { label: 'در حال پردازش و ارسال', color: 'text-primary bg-primary/10 border-primary/20' };
      case 'Delivered':
        return { label: 'تحویل داده شده', color: 'text-emerald-500 bg-emerald-500/10 border-emerald-500/20' };
      case 'ReturnRequest':
        return { label: 'درخواست مرجوعی', color: 'text-rose-500 bg-rose-500/10 border-rose-500/20' };
      case 'Cancelled':
      case 'SystemCancelled':
        return { label: 'لغو شده', color: 'text-muted-foreground bg-muted border-border' };
      default:
        return { label: orderStatus, color: 'text-muted-foreground bg-muted border-border' };
    }
  };

  return (
    <div className="flex w-full flex-col gap-5" dir="rtl">
      
      {/* هدر بخش سفارش‌ها در دسکتاپ */}
      <div className="hidden lg:flex flex-col gap-1 border-b border-border/70 pb-4">
        <h1 className="text-base font-black text-foreground sm:text-lg">سفارش‌های من</h1>
        <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">
          پیگیری وضعیت سفارشات، پرداخت آنلاین فاکتورها و مشاهده تاریخچه مرسولات
        </p>
      </div>

      {/* تب‌های افقی وضعیت سفارش‌ها */}
      <div className="flex w-full items-center gap-2 overflow-x-auto pb-1 scrollbar-none select-none">
        {STATUS_TABS.map((tab) => {
          const TabIcon = tab.icon;
          const isActive = status === tab.id;

          return (
            <button
              key={tab.id}
              type="button"
              onClick={() => handleTabChange(tab.id)}
              className={cn(
                'flex shrink-0 items-center gap-2 rounded-xl border px-3.5 py-2 text-xs font-bold transition-all duration-fast',
                isActive
                  ? 'border-primary bg-primary text-primary-foreground shadow-xs'
                  : 'border-border/80 bg-card text-muted-foreground hover:border-primary/40 hover:text-foreground'
              )}
            >
              <TabIcon className={cn('h-4 w-4', isActive ? 'text-primary-foreground' : 'text-muted-foreground')} />
              <span>{tab.label}</span>
            </button>
          );
        })}
      </div>

      {/* لیست سفارش‌ها */}
      {isLoading ? (
        <PageLoading label="در حال دریافت لیست سفارش‌ها..." />
      ) : orders.length > 0 ? (
        <div className="flex w-full flex-col gap-4">
          <div className="flex w-full flex-col gap-4">
            {orders.map((order: any) => {
              const statusMeta = getOrderStatusMeta(order.status);
              const isWaiting = order.status === 'WaitingForPayment';

              return (
                <Card
                  key={order.id}
                  className="flex w-full flex-col justify-between rounded-2xl border border-border/80 bg-card p-4.5 sm:p-5 shadow-xs transition-colors hover:border-primary/30"
                >
                  {/* ردیف بالا: وضعیت، کد رهگیری، تاریخ و دکمه جزئیات */}
                  <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-3 border-b border-border/60 pb-3.5">
                    <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-3">
                      <span className={cn('rounded-lg border px-2.5 py-1 text-2xs font-bold', statusMeta.color)}>
                        {statusMeta.label}
                      </span>
                      
                      <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 text-xs text-muted-foreground">
                        <span>کد سفارش:</span>
                        <strong className="text-foreground" dir="ltr">
                          #{toPersianDigits(order.orderNumber)}
                        </strong>
                      </div>

                      <span className="hidden sm:inline-block text-border">|</span>

                      <span className="hidden sm:inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 text-2xs text-muted-foreground">
                        <Calendar className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
                        <span>ثبت:</span>
                        <span className="text-foreground font-medium">
                          {order.createDateFormatted || new Date(order.createDate).toLocaleDateString('fa-IR')}
                        </span>
                      </span>
                    </div>

                    <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
                      {isWaiting && order.payableUntil && (
                        <div className="flex items-center gap-2 rounded-xl bg-destructive/10 px-3 py-1">
                          <span className="text-3xs font-bold text-destructive">مهلت پرداخت:</span>
                          <PayableTimer payableUntil={order.payableUntil} />
                        </div>
                      )}

                      <button
                        type="button"
                        onClick={() => router.push(`/profile/orders/${order.id}`)}
                        className="flex items-center gap-1 text-xs font-bold text-primary hover:underline"
                      >
                        <span>مشاهده فاکتور و جزئیات</span>
                        <ChevronLeft className="h-4 w-4" />
                      </button>
                    </div>
                  </div>

                  {/* بدنه تمام‌عرض: عکس اقلام در راست و مبلغ نهایی در چپ */}
                  <div className="mt-4 flex flex-col sm:flex-row sm:items-center sm:justify-between gap-4">
                    <div className="flex flex-col gap-2 min-w-0 flex-1">
                      <span className="text-3xs font-bold text-muted-foreground">
                        اقلام سفارش ({toPersianDigits(order.subOrderItemCount || 1)} کالا):
                      </span>

                      <div className="flex items-center gap-2 overflow-x-auto pb-1 scrollbar-none">
                        {order.shopProductImages && order.shopProductImages.length > 0 ? (
                          order.shopProductImages.map((img: string, idx: number) => (
                            <div
                              key={idx}
                              className="flex h-14 w-14 shrink-0 items-center justify-center overflow-hidden rounded-xl border border-border/70 bg-card p-1 shadow-2xs"
                            >
                              <img
                                src={getFullUrl(img)}
                                className="h-full w-full object-contain"
                                alt=""
                              />
                            </div>
                          ))
                        ) : (
                          <div className="flex h-14 w-14 items-center justify-center rounded-xl border border-border/60 bg-muted">
                            <ImageIcon className="h-6 w-6 text-muted-foreground" />
                          </div>
                        )}
                      </div>
                    </div>

                    <div className="flex items-center justify-between sm:flex-col sm:items-end gap-1.5 shrink-0 pt-3 sm:pt-0 border-t border-dashed border-border/50 sm:border-0">
                      <span className="text-2xs text-muted-foreground">مبلغ نهایی سفارش:</span>
                      <span className="text-base font-black text-foreground" dir="ltr">
                        {order.totalFinalPrice}
                      </span>
                    </div>
                  </div>
                </Card>
              );
            })}
          </div>

          <Pagination
            currentPage={page}
            totalPages={totalPages}
            onPageChange={(p) => {
              setPage(p);
              router.push(`/profile/orders?status=${status}&page=${p}`);
            }}
          />
        </div>
      ) : (
        /* استیت خالی با انیمیشن لاتی اختصاصی */
        <div className="flex w-full flex-col items-center justify-center rounded-2xl border border-dashed border-border/80 bg-card py-12 text-center shadow-xs">
          <div className="mx-auto flex h-48 w-48 max-w-full items-center justify-center -mb-2">
            <LottiePlayer path="/animations/packaging-for-delivery.json" className="h-full w-full" loop={true} />
          </div>
          <p className="text-sm font-bold text-foreground mt-5">
            سفارشی در این بخش وجود ندارد
          </p>
          <p className="mt-1 text-xs text-muted-foreground">
            شما هیچ سفارشی با وضعیت «{STATUS_TABS.find(t => t.id === status)?.label}» ندارید.
          </p>
          <Button
            variant="outline"
            size="sm"
            onClick={() => router.push('/search')}
            className="mt-4 h-9 gap-1.5 rounded-xl px-5 text-xs font-bold shadow-xs"
          >
            <Plus className="h-4 w-4" />
            <span>ثبت سفارش جدید</span>
          </Button>
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

'use client';

import { useRouter } from 'next/navigation';
import { Eye, ArrowRight, ShoppingBag } from 'lucide-react';
import { useGetRecentlyViewedProducts } from '@/domains/userpanel/hooks/userpanel.hooks';
import { ProductSearchCard } from '@/components/features/ProductCard/ProductSearchCard';
import { PageLoading } from '@/components/composites/Loading/PageLoading';
import { Button } from '@/components/primitives/Button/Button';

export function RecentlyViewedList() {
  const router = useRouter();
  const { data: products = [], isLoading } = useGetRecentlyViewedProducts();

  if (isLoading) {
    return <PageLoading label="در حال دریافت آخرین کالاهای بازدید شده..." />;
  }

  return (
    <div className="flex w-full flex-1 flex-col gap-6">
      {/* <div className="mb-1 flex items-center justify-between border-b border-border pb-3 lg:hidden">
        <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => router.push('/profile')}
            aria-label="بازگشت"
            className="-me-1 flex items-center justify-center rounded-full p-1 transition-colors hover:bg-muted"
          >
            <ArrowRight aria-hidden="true" className="h-5 w-5 text-foreground" />
          </button>
          <span className="text-sm font-bold text-foreground">کالاهای دیده شده اخیر</span>
        </div>
      </div> */}

      <div className="flex w-full flex-col gap-2 border-b border-border pb-5">
        <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
          <Eye aria-hidden="true" className="h-5 w-5 shrink-0 text-primary" />
          <span className="text-xl font-bold text-foreground">کالاهای دیده شده اخیر</span>
        </div>
        <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">
          تاریخچه محصولاتی که اخیراً در فروشگاه یدکچی از آن‌ها بازدید کرده‌اید
        </p>
      </div>

      {products.length > 0 ? (
        <div className="grid w-full grid-cols-1 md:gap-3 sm:grid-cols-2 md:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-3 xl:grid-cols-4 2xl:grid-cols-5">
          {products.map((product: any) => (
            <ProductSearchCard key={product.id} product={product} />
          ))}
        </div>
      ) : (
        <div className="flex w-full flex-col items-center justify-center gap-3.5 rounded-lg border border-dashed border-border bg-card py-16 text-center">
          <Eye aria-hidden="true" className="h-12 w-12 text-muted-foreground/60" />
          <span className="text-xs font-bold text-muted-foreground">
            هنوز تاریخچه بازدیدی برای حساب شما ثبت نشده است.
          </span>
          <Button
            variant="outline"
            size="sm"
            onClick={() => router.push('/search')}
            className="mt-2 flex h-10 items-center justify-center gap-1.5 px-6 py-2 text-xs font-bold"
          >
            <ShoppingBag aria-hidden="true" className="h-4 w-4" />
            <span>مشاهده و خرید کالاها</span>
          </Button>
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

'use client';

import * as React from 'react';
import { useRouter } from 'next/navigation';
import { Settings, ArrowRight } from 'lucide-react';
import { useAuth } from '@/domains/auth/hooks/auth.hooks';
import { Card } from '@/components/composites/Card';
import { PageLoading } from '@/components/composites/Loading/PageLoading';
import { PersonalInfoModal } from './components/PersonalInfoModal';
import { ChangeMobileModal } from './components/ChangeMobileModal';
import { DefaultBankModal } from './components/DefaultBankModal';
import { JobModal } from './components/JobModal';
import { EconomicCodeModal } from './components/EconomicCodeModal';
import { LegalInfoModal } from './components/LegalInfoModal';
import { cn } from '@/design-system/utils/cn';

type TabKey = 'personal' | 'legal';

interface SettingItem {
  label: string;
  value: string | null;
  required: boolean;
  modalId: number;
}

export function SettingsForm() {
  const router = useRouter();
  const { user, isLoading } = useAuth();

  const [activeTab, setActiveTab] = React.useState<TabKey>('personal');
  const [activeModal, setActiveModal] = React.useState<number | null>(null);

  if (isLoading || !user) {
    return <PageLoading label="در حال دریافت اطلاعات حساب کاربری..." />;
  }

  const defaultAccount =
    user.bankAccounts?.find((b) => b.isDefault) || user.bankAccounts?.[0] || null;

  const personalItems: SettingItem[] = [
    { label: 'نام و نام خانوادگی', value: user.fullName, required: true, modalId: 1 },
    { label: 'شماره ملی', value: user.nationalCode, required: true, modalId: 1 },
    { label: 'آدرس ایمیل', value: user.email, required: false, modalId: 1 },
    { label: 'شماره موبایل', value: user.phoneNumber, required: true, modalId: 2 },
    {
      label: 'تاریخ تولد',
      value: user.birthDate ? new Date(user.birthDate).toLocaleDateString('fa-IR') : null,
      required: true,
      modalId: 1,
    },
    { label: 'شماره شبا تسویه', value: defaultAccount?.shebaNumber || null, required: true, modalId: 3 },
    { label: 'شماره کارت تسویه', value: defaultAccount?.cardNumber || null, required: true, modalId: 3 },
    { label: 'شغل / سمت', value: user.job, required: false, modalId: 4 },
    {
      label: 'کد اقتصادی اشخاص حقیقی',
      value: user.naturalPersonEconomicCode,
      required: false,
      modalId: 5,
    },
  ];

  const legalItems: SettingItem[] = [
    {
      label: 'نام سازمان / شرکت',
      value: user.legalInfo?.organizationName || null,
      required: true,
      modalId: 6,
    },
    {
      label: 'نوع سازمان / نوع صنف',
      value: user.legalInfo?.organizationType || null,
      required: true,
      modalId: 6,
    },
    {
      label: 'شناسه ملی شرکت / سازمان',
      value: user.legalInfo?.organizationNationalCode || null,
      required: true,
      modalId: 6,
    },
    {
      label: 'شناسه ثبت سازمان',
      value: user.legalInfo?.organizationRegisterationCode || null,
      required: true,
      modalId: 6,
    },
    {
      label: 'کد اقتصادی حقوقی',
      value: user.legalInfo?.organizationEconomicCode || null,
      required: true,
      modalId: 6,
    },
    {
      label: 'استان (محل دفتر مرکزی)',
      value: user.legalInfo?.organizationHeadOfficeProvince || null,
      required: true,
      modalId: 6,
    },
    {
      label: 'شهر (محل دفتر مرکزی)',
      value: user.legalInfo?.organizationHeadOfficeCity || null,
      required: true,
      modalId: 6,
    },
    {
      label: 'شماره تلفن ثابت دفتر مرکزی',
      value: user.legalInfo?.organizationHeadOfficeTel || null,
      required: true,
      modalId: 6,
    },
  ];

  const renderItemCard = (item: SettingItem) => (
    <div key={item.label} className="flex w-full select-none flex-col gap-1.5">
      <span className="me-1 flex items-center gap-1 text-xs font-bold text-muted-foreground">
        {item.label}
        {item.required ? <span className="text-destructive">*</span> : null}
      </span>
      <Card
        onClick={() => setActiveModal(item.modalId)}
        role="button"
        tabIndex={0}
        onKeyDown={(e) => {
          if (e.key === 'Enter' || e.key === ' ') {
            e.preventDefault();
            setActiveModal(item.modalId);
          }
        }}
        className="flex h-11 cursor-pointer items-center justify-start border border-border bg-card px-4 py-2.5 shadow-xs transition-colors duration-fast hover:border-primary/40 hover:bg-accent/50 focus-visible:outline-none focus-visible:ring-2 focus-visible:ring-ring focus-visible:ring-offset-2"
      >
        <span className="truncate text-sm font-medium text-foreground">
          {item.value || <span className="text-muted-foreground">ثبت نشده</span>}
        </span>
      </Card>
    </div>
  );

  return (
    <div className="flex w-full flex-1 flex-col gap-6">
      <div className="mb-1 flex shrink-0 items-center justify-between border-b border-border pb-3 lg:hidden">
        <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => router.push('/profile')}
            aria-label="بازگشت"
            className="-me-1 flex items-center justify-center rounded-full p-1 transition-colors hover:bg-muted"
          >
            <ArrowRight aria-hidden="true" className="h-5 w-5 text-foreground" />
          </button>
          <span className="text-sm font-bold text-foreground">اطلاعات حساب کاربری</span>
        </div>
      </div>

      <div className="flex w-full flex-col gap-2 border-b border-border pb-5">
        <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
          <Settings aria-hidden="true" className="h-5 w-5 shrink-0 text-primary" />
          <span className="text-xl font-bold text-foreground">اطلاعات حساب</span>
        </div>
        <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">
          مشاهده شناسنامه اطلاعات فردی، کارت تسویه، و فاکتور حقوقی شما (برای ویرایش روی هر کارت کلیک کنید)
        </p>
      </div>

      <div className="mt-2 flex w-full select-none items-center gap-5 border-b border-border pb-2.5">
        {(
          [
            { key: 'personal' as const, label: 'مشخصات فردی' },
            { key: 'legal' as const, label: 'اطلاعات حقوقی' },
          ]
        ).map((tab) => (
          <button
            key={tab.key}
            type="button"
            onClick={() => setActiveTab(tab.key)}
            className={cn(
              'border-b-2 pb-2 text-sm font-bold outline-none transition-colors',
              activeTab === tab.key
                ? 'border-primary text-primary'
                : 'border-transparent text-muted-foreground hover:text-foreground'
            )}
          >
            {tab.label}
          </button>
        ))}
      </div>

      <div className="flex w-full flex-col">
        {activeTab === 'personal' ? (
          <div className="grid w-full grid-cols-1 gap-4 md:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-3">
            {personalItems.map(renderItemCard)}
          </div>
        ) : (
          <div className="grid w-full grid-cols-1 gap-4 md:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-3">
            {legalItems.map(renderItemCard)}
          </div>
        )}
      </div>

      <PersonalInfoModal isOpen={activeModal === 1} onClose={() => setActiveModal(null)} user={user} />
      <ChangeMobileModal isOpen={activeModal === 2} onClose={() => setActiveModal(null)} />
      <DefaultBankModal isOpen={activeModal === 3} onClose={() => setActiveModal(null)} />
      <JobModal isOpen={activeModal === 4} onClose={() => setActiveModal(null)} user={user} />
      <EconomicCodeModal isOpen={activeModal === 5} onClose={() => setActiveModal(null)} user={user} />
      <LegalInfoModal isOpen={activeModal === 6} onClose={() => setActiveModal(null)} user={user} />
    </div>
  );
}

FILE: C:\Users\Raven\final-projects\yadakchi-front\yadakchi\src\components\features\Profile\SettingsForm.tsx

'use client';

import * as React from 'react';
import { useRouter } from 'next/navigation';
import { useForm } from 'react-hook-form';
import { zodResolver } from '@hookform/resolvers/zod';
import { z } from 'zod';
import {
  User,
  Building,
  CreditCard,
  Smartphone,
  Save,
} from 'lucide-react';
import { useAuth } from '@/domains/auth/hooks/auth.hooks';
import { useUpdateProfile } from '@/domains/userpanel/hooks/userpanel.hooks';
import { Card } from '@/components/composites/Card';
import { Button } from '@/components/primitives/Button/Button';
import { Input } from '@/components/primitives/Input/Input';
import { Select } from '@/components/primitives/Select/Select';
import { PageLoading } from '@/components/composites/Loading/PageLoading';
import { ChangeMobileModal } from './components/ChangeMobileModal';
import { jalaliToGregorian } from '@/core/utils/formatters';
import { showToast } from '@/core/utils/toast';
import { cn } from '@/design-system/utils/cn';

const profileSchema = z.object({
  fullName: z.string().min(3, 'نام و نام خانوادگی باید حداقل ۳ کاراکتر باشد'),
  // کد ملی کاملاً اختیاری
  nationalCode: z
    .string()
    .refine((val) => !val || /^[0-9]{10}$/.test(val), {
      message: 'کد ملی باید دقیقا ۱۰ رقم عددی باشد',
    })
    .optional()
    .or(z.literal('')),
  email: z.string().email('ایمیل معتبر نیست').optional().or(z.literal('')),
  job: z.string().optional().or(z.literal('')),
  naturalPersonEconomicCode: z.string().optional().or(z.literal('')),

  // اطلاعات حقوقی
  organizationName: z.string().optional().or(z.literal('')),
  organizationType: z.string().optional().or(z.literal('')),
  organizationEconomicCode: z.string().optional().or(z.literal('')),
  organizationNationalCode: z.string().optional().or(z.literal('')),
  organizationRegisterationCode: z.string().optional().or(z.literal('')),
  organizationHeadOfficeTel: z.string().optional().or(z.literal('')),
});

type ProfileFormValues = z.infer<typeof profileSchema>;

export function SettingsForm() {
  const router = useRouter();
  const { user, isLoading } = useAuth();
  const updateProfile = useUpdateProfile();

  const [activeTab, setActiveTab] = React.useState<'personal' | 'legal'>('personal');
  const [isMobileModalOpen, setIsMobileModalOpen] = React.useState(false);

  // وضعیت تاریخ تولد
  const [birthDay, setBirthDay] = React.useState('1');
  const [birthMonth, setBirthMonth] = React.useState('1');
  const [birthYear, setBirthYear] = React.useState('1370');

  const {
    register,
    handleSubmit,
    reset,
    formState: { errors, isDirty },
  } = useForm<ProfileFormValues>({
    resolver: zodResolver(profileSchema),
    defaultValues: {
      fullName: '',
      nationalCode: '',
      email: '',
      job: '',
      naturalPersonEconomicCode: '',
      organizationName: '',
      organizationType: '',
      organizationEconomicCode: '',
      organizationNationalCode: '',
      organizationRegisterationCode: '',
      organizationHeadOfficeTel: '',
    },
  });

  React.useEffect(() => {
    if (user) {
      reset({
        fullName: user.fullName || '',
        nationalCode: user.nationalCode || '',
        email: user.email || '',
        job: user.job || '',
        naturalPersonEconomicCode: user.naturalPersonEconomicCode || '',
        organizationName: user.legalInfo?.organizationName || '',
        organizationType: user.legalInfo?.organizationType || '',
        organizationEconomicCode: user.legalInfo?.organizationEconomicCode || '',
        organizationNationalCode: user.legalInfo?.organizationNationalCode || '',
        organizationRegisterationCode: user.legalInfo?.organizationRegisterationCode || '',
        organizationHeadOfficeTel: user.legalInfo?.organizationHeadOfficeTel || '',
      });

      if (user.birthDate) {
        try {
          const date = new Date(user.birthDate);
          if (!isNaN(date.getTime())) {
            // مقادیر پیش‌فرض تاریخ تولد
            setBirthDay('1');
            setBirthMonth('1');
            setBirthYear('1370');
          }
        } catch {}
      }
    }
  }, [user, reset]);

  if (isLoading && !user) {
    return <PageLoading label="در حال دریافت اطلاعات کاربری..." />;
  }

  const onSubmit = async (data: ProfileFormValues) => {
    const formData = new FormData();
    formData.append('FullName', data.fullName);
    if (data.nationalCode) formData.append('NationalCode', data.nationalCode);
    if (data.email) formData.append('Email', data.email);
    if (data.job) formData.append('Job', data.job);
    if (data.naturalPersonEconomicCode) formData.append('NaturalPersonEconomicCode', data.naturalPersonEconomicCode);

    // افزودن تاریخ تولد
    const birthDateGregorian = jalaliToGregorian(Number(birthYear), Number(birthMonth), Number(birthDay));
    formData.append('BirthDate', birthDateGregorian);

    // افزودن اطلاعات حقوقی در صورت تکمیل نام سازمان
    if (data.organizationName) {
      formData.append('HasLegalInfo', 'true');
      formData.append('OrganizationName', data.organizationName);
      formData.append('OrganizationType', data.organizationType || '');
      formData.append('OrganizationEconomicCode', data.organizationEconomicCode || '');
      formData.append('OrganizationNationalCode', data.organizationNationalCode || '');
      formData.append('OrganizationRegisterationCode', data.organizationRegisterationCode || '');
      formData.append('OrganizationHeadOfficeTel', data.organizationHeadOfficeTel || '');
    }

    try {
      await updateProfile.mutateAsync(formData);
      showToast.success('اطلاعات حساب کاربری با موفقیت بروزرسانی شد');
    } catch {}
  };

  const days = Array.from({ length: 31 }, (_, i) => ({ value: String(i + 1), label: String(i + 1) }));
  const months = [
    { value: '1', label: 'فروردین' },
    { value: '2', label: 'اردیبهشت' },
    { value: '3', label: 'خرداد' },
    { value: '4', label: 'تیر' },
    { value: '5', label: 'مرداد' },
    { value: '6', label: 'شهریور' },
    { value: '7', label: 'مهر' },
    { value: '8', label: 'آبان' },
    { value: '9', label: 'آذر' },
    { value: '10', label: 'دی' },
    { value: '11', label: 'بهمن' },
    { value: '12', label: 'اسفند' },
  ];
  const years = Array.from({ length: 70 }, (_, i) => ({ value: String(1340 + i), label: String(1340 + i) }));

  const defaultAccount = user?.bankAccounts?.find((b: any) => b.isDefault) || user?.bankAccounts?.[0];

  return (
    <div className="flex w-full flex-col gap-5">
      {/* هدر بخش تنظیمات در دسکتاپ */}
      <div className="hidden lg:flex flex-col gap-1 border-b border-border/70 pb-4">
        <h1 className="text-base font-black text-foreground sm:text-lg">اطلاعات حساب کاربری</h1>
        <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">
          اطلاعات هویتی و حقوقی خود را جهت صدور فاکتور رسمی و تسویه سفارشات مدیریت کنید.
        </p>
      </div>

      {/* تب‌های تنظیمات */}
      <div className="flex items-center gap-2 border-b border-border/80">
        <button
          type="button"
          onClick={() => setActiveTab('personal')}
          className={cn(
            'flex items-center gap-2 border-b-2 px-4 py-2.5 text-xs font-bold transition-colors',
            activeTab === 'personal'
              ? 'border-primary text-primary'
              : 'border-transparent text-muted-foreground hover:text-foreground'
          )}
        >
          <User className="h-4 w-4" />
          <span>مشخصات فردی و بانکی</span>
        </button>
        <button
          type="button"
          onClick={() => setActiveTab('legal')}
          className={cn(
            'flex items-center gap-2 border-b-2 px-4 py-2.5 text-xs font-bold transition-colors',
            activeTab === 'legal'
              ? 'border-primary text-primary'
              : 'border-transparent text-muted-foreground hover:text-foreground'
          )}
        >
          <Building className="h-4 w-4" />
          <span>اطلاعات حقوقی و سازمانی</span>
        </button>
      </div>

      {/* کارت تغییر شماره موبایل با امنیت پیامکی */}
      <div className="flex items-center justify-between rounded-2xl border border-primary/20 bg-primary/5 p-4 shadow-2xs">
        <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
          <div className="flex h-10 w-10 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-xl bg-primary/10 text-primary">
            <Smartphone className="h-5 w-5" />
          </div>
          <div className="flex flex-col">
            <span className="text-xs font-bold text-foreground">شماره موبایل فعال</span>
            <span className="text-2xs text-muted-foreground  mt-0.5" dir="ltr">
              {user?.phoneNumber}
            </span>
          </div>
        </div>

        <Button
          type="button"
          variant="outline"
          size="sm"
          onClick={() => setIsMobileModalOpen(true)}
          className="h-8.5 rounded-lg text-2xs font-bold text-primary hover:bg-primary/5"
        >
          تغییر شماره
        </Button>
      </div>

      {/* فرم یکپارچه اصلی */}
      <form onSubmit={handleSubmit(onSubmit)} className="flex flex-col gap-5">
        {activeTab === 'personal' ? (
          <div className="flex flex-col gap-4">
            <Card className="rounded-2xl border border-border/80 bg-card p-5 shadow-xs">
              <h2 className="mb-4 text-xs font-bold text-foreground">شناسنامه هویتی</h2>
              <div className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-4 md:grid-cols-2">
                <Input
                  label="نام و نام خانوادگی *"
                  placeholder="مثال: علی رضایی"
                  error={errors.fullName?.message}
                  {...register('fullName')}
                />

                <Input
                  label="کد ملی (اختیاری)"
                  placeholder="۱۰ رقم بدون خط تیره"
                  maxLength={10}
                  dir="ltr"
                  error={errors.nationalCode?.message}
                  {...register('nationalCode')}
                />

                <Input
                  label="آدرس ایمیل"
                  placeholder="example@gmail.com"
                  dir="ltr"
                  error={errors.email?.message}
                  {...register('email')}
                />

                <Input
                  label="شغل / سمت"
                  placeholder="مثال: مکانیک، فروشنده قطعات"
                  {...register('job')}
                />

                <div className="md:col-span-2">
                  <Input
                    label="کد اقتصادی شخص حقیقی (اختیاری)"
                    placeholder="۱۲ رقم کد اقتصادی"
                    dir="ltr"
                    {...register('naturalPersonEconomicCode')}
                  />
                </div>

                {/* انتخاب تاریخ تولد */}
                <div className="flex flex-col gap-1.5 md:col-span-2">
                  <label className="text-2xs font-bold text-muted-foreground">تاریخ تولد</label>
                  <div className="grid grid-cols-3 gap-2">
                    <Select
                      placeholder="روز"
                      value={birthDay}
                      onChange={(e) => setBirthDay(e.target.value)}
                      options={days}
                    />
                    <Select
                      placeholder="ماه"
                      value={birthMonth}
                      onChange={(e) => setBirthMonth(e.target.value)}
                      options={months}
                    />
                    <Select
                      placeholder="سال"
                      value={birthYear}
                      onChange={(e) => setBirthYear(e.target.value)}
                      options={years}
                    />
                  </div>
                </div>
              </div>
            </Card>

            {/* کارت مشخصات حساب بانکی و تسویه */}
            <Card className="rounded-2xl border border-border/80 bg-card p-5 shadow-xs">
              <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-4">
                <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                  <CreditCard className="h-4.5 w-4.5 text-primary" />
                  <h2 className="text-xs font-bold text-foreground">حساب بانکی پیش‌فرض تسویه</h2>
                </div>
                <Button
                  type="button"
                  variant="outline"
                  size="sm"
                  onClick={() => router.push('/profile/wallet')}
                  className="h-7.5 rounded-lg text-3xs font-bold text-primary"
                >
                  تغییر کارت در کیف پول
                </Button>
              </div>

              {defaultAccount ? (
                <div className="flex flex-col gap-2 rounded-xl bg-muted/40 p-4 border border-border/50 text-xs">
                  <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
                    <span className="text-muted-foreground">شماره کارت:</span>
                    <span className=" font-bold text-foreground" dir="ltr">
                      {defaultAccount.cardNumber}
                    </span>
                  </div>
                  {defaultAccount.shebaNumber && (
                    <div className="flex items-center justify-between border-t border-border/40 pt-2">
                      <span className="text-muted-foreground">شماره شبا:</span>
                      <span className=" font-bold text-foreground" dir="ltr">
                        {defaultAccount.shebaNumber}
                      </span>
                    </div>
                  )}
                </div>
              ) : (
                <p className="text-2xs text-muted-foreground">
                  هنوز شماره کارت یا شبایی برای تسویه در کیف پول ثبت نکرده‌اید.
                </p>
              )}
            </Card>
          </div>
        ) : (
          /* اطلاعات حقوقی */
          <Card className="rounded-2xl border border-border/80 bg-card p-5 shadow-xs">
            <h2 className="mb-4 text-xs font-bold text-foreground">اطلاعات حقوقی شرکت (جهت فاکتور رسمی)</h2>
            <div className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-4 md:grid-cols-2">
              <Input
                label="نام سازمان / شرکت"
                placeholder="نام کامل ثبتی شرکت"
                {...register('organizationName')}
              />

              <Input
                label="نوع سازمان / نوع صنف"
                placeholder="مثال: با مسئولیت محدود، سهامی خاص"
                {...register('organizationType')}
              />

              <Input
                label="شناسه ملی ۱۱ رقمی"
                placeholder="شناسه ملی شرکت"
                maxLength={11}
                dir="ltr"
                {...register('organizationNationalCode')}
              />

              <Input
                label="شناسه ثبت"
                placeholder="شماره ثبت شرکت"
                dir="ltr"
                {...register('organizationRegisterationCode')}
              />

              <Input
                label="کد اقتصادی حقوقی"
                placeholder="کد اقتصادی ۱۲ رقمی"
                maxLength={12}
                dir="ltr"
                {...register('organizationEconomicCode')}
              />

              <Input
                label="تلفن ثابت دفتر مرکزی"
                placeholder="مثال: 02188888888"
                dir="ltr"
                {...register('organizationHeadOfficeTel')}
              />
            </div>
          </Card>
        )}

        {/* دکمه چسبان ذخیره تغییرات */}
        <div className="flex justify-end pt-2">
          <Button
            type="submit"
            variant="primary"
            isLoading={updateProfile.isPending}
            disabled={!isDirty && !updateProfile.isPending}
            className="h-10 gap-2 rounded-xl px-6 text-xs font-bold shadow-sm"
          >
            <Save className="h-4 w-4" />
            <span>ذخیره تغییرات</span>
          </Button>
        </div>
      </form>

      {/* مودال تغییر شماره موبایل */}
      <ChangeMobileModal
        isOpen={isMobileModalOpen}
        onClose={() => setIsMobileModalOpen(false)}
      />
    </div>
  );
}

'use client';

import { useState, useEffect, useRef } from 'react';
import { useRouter } from 'next/navigation';
import { useGetTicketDetails, useSendTicketMessage, useMarkMessagesAsRead } from '@/domains/ticket/hooks/ticket.hooks';
import { Card, CardBody } from '@/components/composites/Card';
import { PageLoading } from '@/components/composites/Loading/PageLoading';
import { Button } from '@/components/primitives/Button/Button';
import { TextArea } from '@/components/primitives/TextArea/TextArea';
import { cn } from '@/design-system/utils/cn';
import { useAuth } from '@/domains/auth/hooks/auth.hooks';
import { ArrowRight, Send, Paperclip, X, FileText, User, Headphones } from 'lucide-react';
import { showToast } from '@/core/utils/toast';

import { getFullUrl } from '@/core/utils/formatters';
interface TicketDetailsProps {
  ticketId: string;
}

export function TicketDetails({ ticketId }: TicketDetailsProps) {
  const router = useRouter();
  const { user } = useAuth();
  const { data: ticket, isLoading } = useGetTicketDetails(ticketId, user?.id);
  const sendMessage = useSendTicketMessage();
  const readMessages = useMarkMessagesAsRead(ticketId);

  const [messageText, setMessageText] = useState('');
  const [attachments, setAttachments] = useState<File[]>([]);
  const messagesEndRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (ticket?.messages) {
      const unreadIds = ticket.messages
        .filter(m => !m.isMyMessage)
        .map(m => m.id);

      if (unreadIds.length > 0) {
        readMessages.mutate(unreadIds);
      }
    }
  }, [ticket]);

  useEffect(() => {
    messagesEndRef.current?.scrollIntoView({ behavior: 'smooth' });
  }, [ticket?.messages]);

  if (isLoading || !ticket) {
    return <PageLoading message="?? ??? ???????? ??????? ????????..." />;
  }

  const handleFileChange = (e: React.ChangeEvent<HTMLInputElement>) => {
    if (e.target.files) {
      const filesArray = Array.from(e.target.files);
      setAttachments(prev => [...prev, ...filesArray].slice(0, 5));
    }
  };

  const handleRemoveFile = (idx: number) => {
    setAttachments(prev => prev.filter((_, i) => i !== idx));
  };

  const handleSendMessage = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();

    if (messageText.trim().length < 2) {
      showToast.error('??? ???? ??? ???? ????? ? ??????? ????');
      return;
    }

    try {
      await sendMessage.mutateAsync({
        ticketId,
        text: messageText,
        attachments,
      });

      showToast.success('???? ??? ?? ?????? ????? ??');
      setMessageText('');
      setAttachments([]);
    } catch (error: any) {
      showToast.error(error.userMessage || '??? ?? ????? ????');
    }
  };
  return (
    <div className="flex-1 flex flex-col h-[calc(100vh-140px)] select-none -mt-6 -mx-4 md:-mx-6 overflow-hidden bg-card" dir="rtl">
      
      <div className="flex items-center justify-between px-4 py-4 border-b shrink-0 bg-muted/20">
        <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
          <button 
            onClick={() => router.push('/profile/support')}
            className="p-1 -me-1 hover:bg-muted rounded-full flex items-center justify-center transition-colors"
            aria-label="Back"
          >
            <ArrowRight className="h-5 w-5 text-foreground" />
          </button>
          <div className="flex flex-col">
            <span className="text-xs font-bold text-foreground block truncate max-w-[200px] sm:max-w-md">{ticket.title}</span>
            <span className="text-[9px] text-muted-foreground block mt-0.5">????? ????: {ticket.ticketNumberFormatted}</span>
          </div>
        </div>
        <span className={cn(
          "px-2.5 py-0.5 rounded-full text-[9px] md:text-[10px] font-bold shrink-0",
          ticket.status === 'WaitingForAnswer' && "bg-warning-50 text-warning-600 dark:bg-warning-950/20 dark:text-warning-400",
          ticket.status === 'Answered' && "bg-success-50 text-success-600 dark:bg-success-950/20 dark:text-success-400",
          ticket.status === 'Closed' && "bg-muted text-muted-foreground"
        )}>
          {ticket.statusLabel}
        </span>
      </div>

      <div className="flex-1 min-h-0 overflow-y-auto p-4 md:p-6 flex flex-col gap-4 no-scrollbar">
        {ticket.messages.length > 0 ? (
          ticket.messages.map((msg) => {
            const isMyMsg = msg.isMyMessage;
            return (
              <div 
                key={msg.id}
                className={cn(
                  "flex items-start gap-3 w-full max-w-[85%] md:max-w-[70%] ",
                  isMyMsg ? "self-start flex-row" : "self-end flex-row-reverse"
                )}
              >
                <div className={cn(
                  "w-8 h-8 rounded-full border flex items-center justify-center shrink-0 shadow-sm",
                  isMyMsg ? "bg-primary/10 border-primary/15 text-primary" : "bg-muted border-border text-muted-foreground"
                )}>
                  {isMyMsg ? <User className="h-4 w-4" /> : <Headphones className="h-4 w-4" />}
                </div>

                <div className="flex flex-col gap-1 w-full min-w-0">
                  <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                    <span className="text-[10px] font-bold text-foreground">{msg.senderName}</span>
                    <span className="text-[8px] text-muted-foreground">{msg.createDateFormatted}</span>
                  </div>
                  <div className={cn(
                    "p-3 rounded-2xl shadow-sm text-xs md:text-sm font-medium leading-relaxed break-words",
                    isMyMsg ? "bg-primary text-primary-foreground rounded-tr-none" : "bg-muted/40 dark:bg-muted border rounded-tl-none text-foreground"
                  )}>
                    {msg.text}
                  </div>

                  {msg.attachments && msg.attachments.length > 0 && (
                    <div className={cn("flex flex-wrap gap-1.5 mt-1.5", isMyMsg ? "justify-start" : "justify-end")}>
                      {msg.attachments.map((url, index) => (
                        <a 
                          key={index}
                          href={getFullUrl(url)}
                          target="_blank"
                          rel="noopener noreferrer"
                          className="h-8 px-2.5 rounded-lg border bg-card text-[9px] font-bold text-foreground flex items-center gap-1.5 shadow-sm hover:border-primary/20"
                        >
                          <FileText className="h-3.5 w-3.5 text-primary shrink-0" />
                          <span className="truncate max-w-[80px]">???? ????? {formatPrice(index + 1)}</span>
                        </a>
                      ))}
                    </div>
                  )}
                </div>
              </div>
            );
          })
        ) : (
          <span className="text-xs text-muted-foreground py-4">????? ??? ???? ???.</span>
        )}
        <div ref={messagesEndRef} />
      </div>

      {ticket.status !== 'Closed' && (
        <div className="border-t bg-card p-4 shrink-0 flex flex-col gap-3.5 relative z-10 w-full shadow-[0_-4px_16px_rgba(0,0,0,0.04)]">
          {attachments.length > 0 && (
            <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-1.5 overflow-x-auto py-0.5 shrink-0 no-scrollbar">
              {attachments.map((file, idx) => (
                <div 
                  key={idx}
                  className="h-8 px-2.5 rounded-lg border bg-muted/20 text-[9px] font-bold text-foreground flex items-center gap-1.5 shrink-0 max-w-[120px]"
                >
                  <FileText className="h-3.5 w-3.5 text-primary shrink-0" />
                  <span className="truncate flex-1">{file.name}</span>
                  <button
                    type="button"
                    onClick={() => handleRemoveFile(idx)}
                    className="p-0.5 hover:bg-muted rounded-full flex items-center justify-center text-muted-foreground hover:text-destructive transition-colors shrink-0"
                  >
                    <X className="h-2.5 w-2.5" />
                  </button>
                </div>
              ))}
            </div>
          )}

          <form onSubmit={handleSendMessage} className="flex gap-2 w-full items-end">
            <label className="h-10 w-10 border rounded-xl hover:bg-muted text-muted-foreground hover:text-primary transition-all shrink-0 cursor-pointer shadow-sm flex items-center justify-center p-2.5">
              <Paperclip className="h-4.5 w-4.5" />
              <input
                type="file"
                multiple
                accept="image/*,application/pdf"
                onChange={handleFileChange}
                className="hidden"
              />
            </label>

            <div className="flex-1 min-w-0">
              <TextArea
                placeholder="???? ??? ?? ????? ???????..."
                value={messageText}
                onChange={(e) => setMessageText(e.target.value)}
                className="h-10 py-2.5 text-xs leading-normal"
                required
              />
            </div>

            <Button
              type="submit"
              variant="primary"
              size="sm"
              isLoading={sendMessage.isPending}
              className="rounded-xl h-10 w-10 shrink-0 p-2.5 flex items-center justify-center shadow-md shadow-primary/10"
              aria-label="Send"
            >
              {!sendMessage.isPending && <Send className="h-4 w-4 transform rotate-180" />}
            </Button>
          </form>
        </div>
      )}

    </div>
  );
}

const formatPrice = (value: number) => {
  return new Intl.NumberFormat('fa-IR').format(value);
};

  const closedCount = allTickets.filter((t) => t.status === 'Closed').length;

  ];

'use client';

import * as React from 'react';
import { useRouter } from 'next/navigation';
import { motion } from 'framer-motion';
import {
  Wallet,
  CreditCard,
  Clock,
  ArrowUpRight,
  ArrowDownLeft,
  Scale,
  Plus,
  Wifi,
  Sparkles,
  ChevronLeft,
} from 'lucide-react';
import {
  useGetWalletBalances,
  useGetTransactions,
  useGetWithdrawRequests,
} from '@/domains/userpanel/hooks/userpanel.hooks';
import { Card } from '@/components/composites/Card';
import { Button } from '@/components/primitives/Button/Button';
import { Select } from '@/components/primitives/Select/Select';
import { PageLoading } from '@/components/composites/Loading/PageLoading';
import { Pagination } from '@/components/composites/Pagination/Pagination';
import { LottiePlayer } from '@/components/shared/Lottie/LottiePlayer';
import { WithdrawModal } from './components/WithdrawModal';
import { toPersianDigits } from '@/core/utils/formatters';
import { cn } from '@/design-system/utils/cn';

const STATUS_OPTIONS = [
  { value: '', label: 'همه وضعیت‌ها' },
  { value: 'Pending', label: 'در انتظار پرداخت' },
  { value: 'Paid', label: 'تسویه شده' },
  { value: 'Cancelled', label: 'لغو شده' },
] as const;

export function WalletDashboard() {
  const router = useRouter();

  const [activeTab, setActiveTab] = React.useState<'withdraws' | 'transactions'>('withdraws');
  const [withdrawStatus, setWithdrawStatus] = React.useState('');
  const [withdrawPage, setWithdrawPage] = React.useState(1);
  const [transactionPage, setTransactionPage] = React.useState(1);
  const [isWithdrawModalOpen, setIsWithdrawModalOpen] = React.useState(false);

  const { data: wallet, isLoading: isWalletLoading } = useGetWalletBalances();
  const { data: transactionsResponse, isLoading: isTransactionsLoading } = useGetTransactions(transactionPage);
  const { data: withdrawsResponse, isLoading: isWithdrawsLoading } = useGetWithdrawRequests(withdrawPage, withdrawStatus);

  const transactions = transactionsResponse?.items || [];
  const transactionsTotalPages = transactionsResponse?.totalPages || 1;
  const withdraws = withdrawsResponse?.items || [];
  const withdrawsTotalPages = withdrawsResponse?.totalPages || 1;

  if (isWalletLoading) {
    return <PageLoading label="در حال دریافت اطلاعات کیف پول..." />;
  }

  const getWithdrawStatusMeta = (status: string, statusLabel: string) => {
    switch (status) {
      case 'Paid':
        return { label: statusLabel || 'تسویه شده', color: 'text-emerald-500 bg-emerald-500/10 border-emerald-500/20' };
      case 'Pending':
        return { label: statusLabel || 'در حال بررسی', color: 'text-amber-500 bg-amber-500/10 border-amber-500/20' };
      case 'Cancelled':
        return { label: statusLabel || 'لغو شده', color: 'text-rose-500 bg-rose-500/10 border-rose-500/20' };
      default:
        return { label: statusLabel || status, color: 'text-muted-foreground bg-muted border-border' };
    }
  };

  return (
    <div className="flex w-full flex-col gap-5" dir="rtl">
      
      {/* ================= 1. کارت VIP متالیک با انیمیشن شاینینگ (Titanium & Copper Smart Card) ================= */}
      <div className="relative overflow-hidden rounded-3xl border border-white/10 bg-gradient-to-br from-zinc-950 via-[#18191D] to-zinc-900 p-6 sm:p-7 text-white shadow-2xl shadow-black/40">
        
        {/* هاله نور مس متالیک در گوشه کارت جهت حفظ تم برند */}
        <div className="absolute -top-16 -end-16 h-56 w-56 rounded-full bg-primary/25 blur-3xl pointer-events-none" />
        <div className="absolute -bottom-20 -start-20 h-56 w-56 rounded-full bg-primary/10 blur-3xl pointer-events-none" />

        {/* انیمیشن براق شاینینگ شبیه به بازتاب نور روی فلز کارت */}
        <motion.div
          initial={{ x: '-150%' }}
          animate={{ x: '200%' }}
          transition={{
            repeat: Infinity,
            duration: 3,
            ease: 'easeInOut',
            repeatDelay: 2.5,
          }}
          className="pointer-events-none absolute inset-0 -skew-x-20 bg-gradient-to-r from-transparent via-white/10 to-transparent"
        />

        <div className="relative z-10 flex flex-col justify-between gap-6">
          {/* هدر کارت: چیپ هوشمند، لوگو و دکمه قوانین */}
          <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
            <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
              {/* شبیه‌ساز چیپ طلایی کارت اعتباری */}
              <div className="flex h-8 w-11 items-center justify-center rounded-lg border border-amber-400/30 bg-gradient-to-br from-amber-200/20 via-amber-400/30 to-amber-600/20 shadow-inner">
                <div className="h-4 w-7 rounded-xs border border-amber-300/40 opacity-75" />
              </div>

              <Wifi className="h-5 w-5 text-white/50 rotate-90" />
              <span className="text-xs font-black tracking-widest text-white/80">کیف پول</span>
            </div>

            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => router.push('/terms?section=wallet')}
              className="flex items-center gap-1.5 rounded-xl border border-white/10 bg-white/5 px-3 py-1.5 text-3xs font-bold text-white/80 backdrop-blur-md hover:bg-white/15 transition-all"
            >
              <Scale className="h-3.5 w-3.5 text-primary" />
              <span>قوانین تسویه</span>
            </button>
          </div>

          {/* موجودی کل با تایپوگرافی شارپ پلاتینیومی */}
          <div className="flex flex-col gap-1.5">
            <span className="text-3xs font-bold tracking-wider text-white/60">موجودی کل حساب شما</span>
            <div className="flex items-baseline gap-2">
              <span className="text-3xl sm:text-4xl font-black text-white tracking-tight">
                {wallet?.totalBalance || '۰ تومان'}
              </span>
            </div>
          </div>

          {/* نوار پایین کارت: موجودی قابل برداشت و دکمه تسویه */}
          <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-4 border-t border-white/10 pt-4">
            <div className="flex flex-col">
              <span className="text-3xs text-white/60">موجودی قابل برداشت:</span>
              <span className="text-sm font-bold text-emerald-400 mt-0.5">
                {wallet?.withdrawableBalance || '۰ تومان'}
              </span>
            </div>

            <Button
              variant="primary"
              size="sm"
              onClick={() => setIsWithdrawModalOpen(true)}
              className="h-10 gap-2 rounded-xl px-5 text-xs font-black shadow-lg shadow-primary/30 transition-transform active:scale-95"
            >
              <CreditCard className="h-4 w-4" />
              <span>ثبت درخواست برداشت وجه</span>
            </Button>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* ================= 2. تب‌های کپسولی سوئیچ بین برداشت‌ها و تراکنش‌ها ================= */}
      <div className="flex flex-col gap-3 rounded-2xl border border-border/80 bg-card p-3 shadow-2xs sm:flex-row sm:items-center sm:justify-between select-none">
        <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 rounded-xl bg-muted/60 p-1">
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => setActiveTab('withdraws')}
            className={cn(
              'rounded-lg px-3.5 py-2 text-xs font-bold transition-all',
              activeTab === 'withdraws'
                ? 'bg-card text-foreground shadow-xs'
                : 'text-muted-foreground hover:text-foreground'
            )}
          >
            درخواست‌های تسویه ({toPersianDigits(withdraws.length)})
          </button>
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => setActiveTab('transactions')}
            className={cn(
              'rounded-lg px-3.5 py-2 text-xs font-bold transition-all',
              activeTab === 'transactions'
                ? 'bg-card text-foreground shadow-xs'
                : 'text-muted-foreground hover:text-foreground'
            )}
          >
            گردش و تراکنش‌های مالی
          </button>
        </div>

        {activeTab === 'withdraws' && (
          <div className="w-full sm:w-44">
            <Select
              placeholder="فیلتر وضعیت"
              value={withdrawStatus}
              onChange={(e) => {
                setWithdrawStatus(e.target.value);
                setWithdrawPage(1);
              }}
              options={[...STATUS_OPTIONS]}
            />
          </div>
        )}
      </div>

      {/* ================= 3. محتوای تب درخواست‌های برداشت ================= */}
      {activeTab === 'withdraws' ? (
        <div className="flex flex-col gap-4">
          {isWithdrawsLoading ? (
            <PageLoading label="در حال دریافت درخواست‌های برداشت..." />
          ) : withdraws.length > 0 ? (
            <div className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-4 md:grid-cols-2">
              {withdraws.map((w: any) => {
                const meta = getWithdrawStatusMeta(w.status, w.statusLabel);

                return (
                  <Card key={w.id} className="flex flex-col justify-between rounded-2xl border border-border/80 bg-card p-4.5 sm:p-5 shadow-xs">
                    <div>
                      <div className="flex items-center justify-between border-b border-border/50 pb-3">
                        <div className="flex flex-col">
                          <span className="text-3xs text-muted-foreground">مبلغ درخواستی تسویه:</span>
                          <span className="text-base font-black text-foreground mt-0.5">{w.amount}</span>
                        </div>
                        <span className={cn('rounded-lg border px-2.5 py-1 text-2xs font-bold', meta.color)}>
                          {meta.label}
                        </span>
                      </div>

                      <div className="mt-3 flex flex-col gap-2 text-xs text-muted-foreground">
                        {w.cardNumber && (
                          <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
                            <span className="flex items-center gap-1.5">
                              <CreditCard className="h-3.5 w-3.5 text-muted-foreground" />
                              واریز به کارت:
                            </span>
                            <span className="font-bold text-foreground " dir="ltr">
                              {w.cardNumber}
                            </span>
                          </div>
                        )}
                        <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
                          <span className="flex items-center gap-1.5">
                            <Clock className="h-3.5 w-3.5 text-muted-foreground" />
                            زمان ثبت:
                          </span>
                          <span className="font-bold text-foreground">{w.createDateFormatted}</span>
                        </div>
                      </div>
                    </div>
                  </Card>
                );
              })}
            </div>
          ) : (
            <div className="flex w-full flex-col items-center justify-center rounded-2xl border border-dashed border-border/80 bg-card py-12 text-center shadow-xs">
              <div className="mx-auto flex h-48 w-48 max-w-full items-center justify-center -mb-2">
                <LottiePlayer path="/anumations/money-with-wings.json" className="h-full w-full" loop={true} />
              </div>
              <p className="text-sm font-bold text-foreground mt-5">
                هیچ درخواست برداشتی در این دسته یافت نشد.
              </p>
              <p className="mt-1 text-xs text-muted-foreground">
                شما تاکنون درخواست تسویه حسابی با این وضعیت ثبت نکرده‌اید.
              </p>
              {/* <Button
                variant="outline"
                size="sm"
                onClick={() => setIsWithdrawModalOpen(true)}
                className="mt-4 h-9 gap-1.5 rounded-xl px-5 text-xs font-bold shadow-xs"
              >
                <Plus className="h-4 w-4" />
                <span>ثبت درخواست برداشت وجه</span>
              </Button> */}
            </div>
          )}

          <Pagination
            currentPage={withdrawPage}
            totalPages={withdrawsTotalPages}
            onPageChange={(p) => setWithdrawPage(p)}
          />
        </div>
      ) : (
        /* ================= 4. محتوای تب تراکنش‌های مالی ================= */
        <div className="flex flex-col gap-4">
          {isTransactionsLoading ? (
            <PageLoading label="در حال دریافت گردش حساب..." />
          ) : transactions.length > 0 ? (
            <div className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-4 md:grid-cols-2">
              {transactions.map((t: any, idx: number) => (
                <Card key={idx} className="flex flex-col justify-between rounded-2xl border border-border/80 bg-card p-4.5 sm:p-5 shadow-xs">
                  <div>
                    <div className="flex items-center justify-between border-b border-border/50 pb-3">
                      <div className="flex items-center gap-2.5">
                        <div className={cn(
                          'flex h-9 w-9 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-xl',
                          t.isSuccess ? 'bg-emerald-500/10 text-emerald-500' : 'bg-rose-500/10 text-rose-500'
                        )}>
                          {t.isSuccess ? <ArrowDownLeft className="h-4.5 w-4.5" /> : <ArrowUpRight className="h-4.5 w-4.5" />}
                        </div>
                        <div className="flex flex-col">
                          <span className="text-xs font-black text-foreground">{t.typeLabel}</span>
                          <span className="text-3xs text-muted-foreground">{t.createDateFormatted}</span>
                        </div>
                      </div>

                      <span className={cn(
                        'rounded-lg px-2.5 py-1 text-2xs font-bold',
                        t.isSuccess ? 'bg-emerald-500/10 text-emerald-600 dark:text-emerald-400' : 'bg-rose-500/10 text-rose-500'
                      )}>
                        {t.isSuccess ? 'موفق' : 'ناموفق'}
                      </span>
                    </div>

                    <div className="mt-3 flex flex-col gap-2 text-xs">
                      <div className="flex items-center justify-between text-muted-foreground">
                        <span>مبلغ تراکنش:</span>
                        <span className="font-black text-foreground text-sm">{t.amount}</span>
                      </div>
                      {t.traceNo && (
                        <div className="flex items-center justify-between text-muted-foreground border-t border-dashed border-border/50 pt-2">
                          <span>شماره پیگیری:</span>
                          <span className="font-bold text-foreground " dir="ltr">{t.traceNo}</span>
                        </div>
                      )}
                    </div>
                  </div>
                </Card>
              ))}
            </div>
          ) : (
            <div className="flex w-full flex-col items-center justify-center rounded-2xl border border-dashed border-border/80 bg-card py-12 text-center shadow-xs">
              <div className="mx-auto flex h-48 w-48 max-w-full items-center justify-center -mb-2">
                <LottiePlayer path="/anumations/money-with-wings.json" className="h-full w-full" loop={true} />
              </div>
              <p className="text-sm font-bold text-foreground mt-5">
                هیچ تراکنشی در حساب شما ثبت نشده است.
              </p>
              <p className="mt-1 text-xs text-muted-foreground">
                گردش حساب و تراکنش‌های مالی شما در این بخش نمایش داده خواهند شد.
              </p>
            </div>
          )}

          <Pagination
            currentPage={transactionPage}
            totalPages={transactionsTotalPages}
            onPageChange={(p) => setTransactionPage(p)}
          />
        </div>
      )}

      {/* مودال درخواست برداشت وجه */}
      <WithdrawModal
        isOpen={isWithdrawModalOpen}
        onClose={() => setIsWithdrawModalOpen(false)}
        wallet={wallet}
      />
    </div>
  );
}

'use client';

import { useQueryClient } from '@tanstack/react-query';
import { useTypedQuery, useTypedMutation } from '@/lib/react-query/hooks/base.hooks';
import { getUserPanelService } from '../services/userpanel.service';
import { OrderListItemViewModel, WalletViewModel, TransactionViewModel, UserVehicleViewModel, NotificationItemViewModel, WithdrawRequestItemViewModel } from '../types/view.types';
import { PaginatedResult } from '@/shared/types/common.types';
import { OrderDetailsDto, BankAccountDto } from '../types/dto.types';

const userPanelService = getUserPanelService();

export function usePostShopRequest() {
  return useTypedMutation(
    () => userPanelService.postShopRequest()
  );
}

export function useGetOrderDetails(orderId: string) {
  return useTypedQuery<OrderDetailsDto>(
    ['user', 'orders', 'details', orderId],
    () => userPanelService.getOrder(orderId),
    { enabled: !!orderId }
  );
}

export function useGetOrders(status?: string, searchedValue?: string, orderBy?: string, pageNumber: number = 1) {
  return useTypedQuery<PaginatedResult<OrderListItemViewModel>>(
    ['user', 'orders', 'list', { status, searchedValue, orderBy, pageNumber }],
    () => userPanelService.getOrders(status, searchedValue, orderBy, pageNumber, 10),
    { staleTime: 30 * 1000 }
  );
}

export function useGetOrderReceipt(orderId: string) {
  return useTypedQuery<any>(
    ['user', 'orders', 'receipt', orderId],
    () => userPanelService.getOrderReceipt(orderId),
    { enabled: !!orderId }
  );
}

// اصلاح هوک پرداخت مجدد برای قبول و ارسال پاسخ حاوی لینک درگاه بانکی برگشت داده شده از سرویس
export function useRetryOrderPayment() {
  return useTypedMutation(
    (orderId: string) => userPanelService.retryOrderPayment(orderId)
  );
}

export function useGetSubOrderCancelReasons() {
  return useTypedQuery<any[]>(
    ['user', 'orders', 'cancel-reasons'],
    () => userPanelService.getSubOrderCancelReasons(),
    { staleTime: 10 * 60 * 1000 }
  );
}

export function useCancelOrder() {
  const queryClient = useQueryClient();
  return useTypedMutation(
    ({ orderId, cancelationReasonId, userCancelationDescription }: { orderId: string; cancelationReasonId: string; userCancelationDescription: string }) =>
      userPanelService.cancelOrder(orderId, cancelationReasonId, userCancelationDescription),
    {
      onSuccess: () => {
        queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ['user', 'orders'] });
      }
    }
  );
}

export function useMarkSubOrderReceived() {
  const queryClient = useQueryClient();
  return useTypedMutation(
    (subOrderId: string) => userPanelService.markSubOrderReceived(subOrderId),
    {
      onSuccess: () => {
        queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ['user', 'orders'] });
      }
    }
  );
}

export function useMarkSubOrderNotReceived() {
  const queryClient = useQueryClient();
  return useTypedMutation(
    (subOrderId: string) => userPanelService.markSubOrderNotReceived(subOrderId),
    {
      onSuccess: () => {
        queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ['user', 'orders'] });
      }
    }
  );
}

export function useSubmitSubOrderItemFeedback() {
  return useTypedMutation(
    (payload: any) => userPanelService.submitSubOrderItemFeedback(payload)
  );
}

export function useGetReturnRequestReasons() {
  return useTypedQuery<any[]>(
    ['user', 'return-requests', 'reasons'],
    () => userPanelService.getReturnRequestReasons(),
    { staleTime: 10 * 60 * 1000 }
  );
}

export function useSubmitReturnRequest() {
  const queryClient = useQueryClient();
  return useTypedMutation(
    ({ subOrderId, items, files }: { subOrderId: string; items: any[]; files?: File[] }) =>
      userPanelService.submitReturnRequest(subOrderId, items, files),
    {
      onSuccess: () => {
        queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ['user', 'return-requests'] });
        queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ['user', 'orders'] });
      }
    }
  );
}

export function useGetReturnRequestDetails(id: string) {
  return useTypedQuery<any>(
    ['user', 'return-requests', 'details', id],
    () => userPanelService.getReturnRequestDetails(id),
    { enabled: !!id }
  );
}

export function useGetReturnRequests(pageNumber: number = 1, status?: string, subOrderId?: string) {
  return useTypedQuery<PaginatedResult<any>>(
    ['user', 'return-requests', 'list', { pageNumber, status, subOrderId }],
    () => userPanelService.getReturnRequests(pageNumber, 10, status, subOrderId),
    { staleTime: 30 * 1000 }
  );
}

export function useSubmitReturnShipmentReceipt() {
  const queryClient = useQueryClient();
  return useTypedMutation(
    ({ id, method, traceNumber, file }: { id: string; method: string; traceNumber: string; file: File }) =>
      userPanelService.submitReturnShipmentReceipt(id, method, traceNumber, file),
    {
      onSuccess: () => {
        queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ['user', 'return-requests'] });
      }
    }
  );
}

export function useGetWalletBalances() {
  return useTypedQuery<WalletViewModel>(
    ['user', 'wallet', 'balances'],
    () => userPanelService.getWalletBalances(),
    { staleTime: 30 * 1000 }
  );
}

export function useGetTransactions(pageNumber: number = 1) {
  return useTypedQuery<PaginatedResult<TransactionViewModel>>(
    ['user', 'wallet', 'transactions', pageNumber],
    () => userPanelService.getTransactions(pageNumber, 10),
    { staleTime: 30 * 1000 }
  );
}

export function useGetBankAccounts() {
  return useTypedQuery<BankAccountDto[]>(
    ['user', 'bank-accounts', 'list'],
    () => userPanelService.getBankAccounts(),
    { staleTime: 5 * 60 * 1000 }
  );
}

export function useCreateBankAccount() {
  const queryClient = useQueryClient();
  return useTypedMutation(
    ({ cardNumber, shebaNumber }: { cardNumber: string; shebaNumber: string }) =>
      userPanelService.createBankAccount(cardNumber, shebaNumber),
    {
      onSuccess: () => {
        queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ['user', 'bank-accounts'] });
      }
    }
  );
}

export function useSetDefaultBankAccount() {
  const queryClient = useQueryClient();
  return useTypedMutation(
    (bankAccountId: string) => userPanelService.setDefaultBankAccount(bankAccountId),
    {
      onSuccess: () => {
        queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ['user', 'bank-accounts'] });
      }
    }
  );
}

export function useGetUserVehicles() {
  return useTypedQuery<UserVehicleViewModel[]>(
    ['user', 'vehicles', 'list'],
    () => userPanelService.getUserVehicles(),
    { staleTime: 5 * 60 * 1000 }
  );
}

export function useCreateUserVehicle() {
  const queryClient = useQueryClient();
  return useTypedMutation(
    ({ carId, title, isDefault }: { carId: string; title: string; isDefault: boolean }) =>
      userPanelService.createUserVehicle(carId, title, isDefault),
    {
      onSuccess: () => {
        queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ['user', 'vehicles'] });
      }
    }
  );
}

export function useUpdateUserVehicle() {
  const queryClient = useQueryClient();
  return useTypedMutation(
    ({ id, title, mileage, oilKmLimit, lastServiceDate, isDefault }: { id: string; title: string; mileage: number | null; oilKmLimit: number | null; lastServiceDate: string | null; isDefault: boolean }) =>
      userPanelService.updateUserVehicle(id, title, mileage, oilKmLimit, lastServiceDate, isDefault),
    {
      onSuccess: () => {
        queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ['user', 'vehicles'] });
      }
    }
  );
}

export function useDeleteUserVehicle() {
  const queryClient = useQueryClient();
  return useTypedMutation(
    (id: string) => userPanelService.deleteUserVehicle(id),
    {
      onSuccess: () => {
        queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ['user', 'vehicles'] });
      }
    }
  );
}

export function useGetUserNotifications(pageNumber: number = 1, pageSize: number = 30, channel?: string, priority?: string, isRead?: boolean, orderBy?: 'Latest' | 'Oldest') {
  return useTypedQuery<PaginatedResult<NotificationItemViewModel>>(
    ['user', 'notifications', 'list', { pageNumber, channel, priority, isRead, orderBy }],
    () => userPanelService.getUserNotifications(pageNumber, 10, channel, priority, undefined, undefined, undefined, isRead, orderBy),
    { staleTime: 30 * 1000 }
  );
}

export function useGetNotificationDetails(id: string) {
  return useTypedQuery<any>(
    ['user', 'notifications', 'details', id],
    () => userPanelService.getNotificationDetails(id),
    { enabled: !!id }
  );
}

export function useGetShopAdvantages() {
  return useTypedQuery<any[]>(
    ['user', 'shop-feedback', 'advantages'],
    () => userPanelService.getShopAdvantages(),
    { staleTime: 10 * 60 * 1000 }
  );
}

export function useGetShopDisadvantages() {
  return useTypedQuery<any[]>(
    ['user', 'shop-feedback', 'disadvantages'],
    () => userPanelService.getShopDisadvantages(),
    { staleTime: 10 * 60 * 1000 }
  );
}

export function useUpdateProfile() {
  const queryClient = useQueryClient();
  return useTypedMutation(
    (formData: FormData) => userPanelService.updateProfile(formData),
    {
      onSuccess: () => {
        queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ['auth', 'user'] });
      }
    }
  );
}

export function useGetWithdrawRequests(pageNumber: number = 1, status?: string) {
  return useTypedQuery<PaginatedResult<WithdrawRequestItemViewModel>>(
    ['user', 'wallet', 'withdraw-requests', { pageNumber, status }],
    () => userPanelService.getWithdrawRequests(pageNumber, 10, status),
    { staleTime: 30 * 1000 }
  );
}

export function useSubmitWithdrawRequest() {
  const queryClient = useQueryClient();
  return useTypedMutation(
    ({ amount, bankAccountId }: { amount: number; bankAccountId: string }) =>
      userPanelService.submitWithdrawRequest(amount, bankAccountId),
    {
      onSuccess: () => {
        queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ['user', 'wallet'] });
      }
    }
  );
}

export function useGetFavoriteProducts(pageNumber: number = 1, order: string = 'Latest') {
  return useTypedQuery<PaginatedResult<any>>(
    ['user', 'favorites', 'list', { pageNumber, order }],
    () => userPanelService.getFavoriteProducts(pageNumber, 30, order),
    { staleTime: 30 * 1000 }
  );
}

export function useGetRecentlyViewedProducts() {
  return useTypedQuery<any[]>(
    ['user', 'recent-views', 'list'],
    () => userPanelService.getRecentlyViewedProducts(),
    { staleTime: 30 * 1000 }
  );
}

export function useRequestUpdatePhoneNumber() {
  return useTypedMutation(
    (phoneNumber: string) => userPanelService.requestUpdatePhoneNumber(phoneNumber)
  );
}

export function useConfirmCurrentPhoneOTP() {
  return useTypedMutation(
    (otpCode: string) => userPanelService.confirmCurrentPhoneOTP(otpCode)
  );
}

export function useConfirmNewPhoneOTP() {
  return useTypedMutation(
    (otpCode: string) => userPanelService.confirmNewPhoneOTP(otpCode)
  );
}
